### [1] [Intermittent Strategic Cooperation of Two Selfish Agents on Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17216)
*Itay Shedlezki,Noa Agmon*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究两个自利代理在时空约束下的间歇性策略合作路径规划问题，证明了纯纳什均衡的存在性，并提出了多项式时间算法来枚举所有相关均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究自利代理在路径规划中的策略合作问题，这种合作虽能严格惠及双方，但存在策略脆弱性——代理可能随时偏离合作路径，因此需要理解稳定合作的条件及均衡结构。

Method: 将间歇性策略合作路径规划（IC2PP）建模为图上的双人博弈；刻画纯纳什均衡联合策略的结构特征；提出多项式时间算法枚举所有相关PNE；采用基于讨价还价理论的选择概念作为协调机制。

Result: 证明了稳定合作必须遵循高度受限的形式；每个IC2PP实例至少存在一个纯纳什均衡；提出了多项式时间算法枚举所有相关PNE；在多重均衡情形下，通过实证比较了不同均衡结果在个体行程时间和社会福利方面的表现。

Conclusion: IC2PP问题中稳定的策略合作具有严格的结构约束，纯纳什均衡必然存在且可有效计算；当存在多重均衡时，基于讨价还价理论的协调机制能够帮助代理选择合理的合作方案。

Abstract: We study strategic space- and time-constrained cooperation between two self-interested agents through the Intermittent Strategic Cooperation-Based Two-Agent Path Planning (IC2PP) problem, a shortest-path game on graphs in which agents navigate toward individual targets while optionally cooperating at specific nodes to reduce their own travel times. Although such cooperation can strictly benefit both agents, it is strategically fragile: agents may deviate at any point along their paths. Modeled as a 2-player game, we characterize the structure of Pure Nash Equilibrium (PNE) joint strategies in IC2PP, and show that stable cooperation must follow a highly constrained form. We further prove that at least one PNE exists in every instance of IC2PP, and present a polynomial-time algorithm for enumerating all relevant PNEs. When multiple equilibria arise, we study coordination mechanisms based on bargaining-theoretic selection concepts and empirically compare equilibrium outcomes in terms of individual travel times and social welfare.

</details>


### [2] [Trustworthy Self-Composable Big-Data-as-a-Service: An LLM-Orchestrated Multi-Agent Framework for Automated Data Engineering, AutoML, MLOps Deployment, and Drift-Aware Lifecycle Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17915)
*Aueaphum Aueawatthanaphisut,Badri Raj Lamichhane*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM编排多智能体协作的可信自组合BDaaS框架，实现了从数据摄取到漂移检测的全生命周期自动化，在预测性能、工件追溯性、可复现性和漂移恢复方面均优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的数据科学智能体和AutoML系统主要关注孤立的工作流阶段，缺乏对全生命周期编排、工件治理、人工监督和漂移感知适应的支持，无法满足BDaaS平台对跨数据摄取、清洗、特征工程、模型开发、部署及后部署监控的可靠自动化需求。

Method: 提出基于LLM编排的多智能体协作架构，将BDaaS生命周期分解为数据摄取、数据清洗、特征工程、AutoML训练、模型评估、MLOps部署、监控和漂移检测等专用智能体。中央LLM编排层协调智能体执行、验证中间输出、管理工作流上下文并支持动态工作流组合。框架还集成了共享工件治理、可复现性支持、人在环检查点和漂移感知反馈循环。使用带有缺失值、分类变量、异常值、类别不平衡和模拟协变量漂移的控制表格基准数据集进行基于原型的评估。

Result: 与手动ML、纯AutoML和单智能体LLM基线相比，所提出的多智能体BDaaS流水线实现了具有竞争力的预测性能，同时在生命周期级别的可靠性方面有所提升，包括工作流完成度、工件可追溯性、部署就绪性、可复现性和漂移恢复能力。

Conclusion: 研究表明，LLM编排的多智能体系统可以将传统AutoML扩展为可信赖、自适应且面向生产的BDaaS生命周期自动化解决方案，为构建生产级大数据服务平台提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Big-Data-as-a-Service (BDaaS) platforms require re liable automation across data ingestion, cleaning, feature engi neering, model development, deployment, and post-deployment monitoring. However, existing LLM-based data science agents and AutoML systems mainly focus on isolated workflow stages, leaving limited support for lifecycle-level orchestration, artifact governance, human oversight, and drift-aware adaptation. This paper proposes a trustworthy self-composable BDaaS frame work based on LLM-orchestrated multi-agent collaboration. The proposed architecture decomposes the BDaaS lifecycle into specialized agents for data ingestion, data cleaning, feature engineering, AutoML training, model evaluation, MLOps de ployment, monitoring, and drift detection. A central LLM or chestration layer coordinates agent execution, validates interme diate outputs, manages workflow context, and enables dynamic workflow composition. The framework also incorporates shared artifact governance, reproducibility support, human-in-the-loop checkpoints, and drift-aware feedback loops. A prototype-based evaluation is conducted using controlled tabular benchmark datasets with missing values, categorical variables, outliers, class imbalance, and simulated covariate drift. Compared with manual ML, AutoML-only, and single-agent LLM baselines, the pro posed multi-agent BDaaS pipeline achieves competitive predictive performance while improving lifecycle-level reliability, including workflow completion, artifact traceability, deployment readiness, reproducibility, and drift recovery. The results suggest that LLM-orchestrated multi-agent systems can extend conventional AutoML toward trustworthy, adaptive, and production-oriented BDaaS lifecycle automation.

</details>


### [3] [A Neuro-Symbolic Approach to Strategy Synthesis for Strategic Logics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17962)
*Marco Aruta,Vadim Malvone,Aniello Murano,Domenico Parente,Luca Rizzuti*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经符号框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）作为策略生成预言机，结合形式化模型检验器验证候选策略，解决了多智能体系统中策略合成的计算成本问题，在NatATL策略合成任务上达到92%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统（MAS）中关于战略交互的推理是一个核心挑战。现有的战略能力逻辑（如ATL）虽然提供了严格的方法，但其策略合成的计算成本往往阻碍了实际应用。需要一种方法能在保持形式化正确性的前提下，高效地导航大规模组合策略空间。

Method: 提出了一种神经符号框架，将大语言模型（LLM）集成到MAS模型检验流程中。LLM作为策略生成预言机，提出候选策略；标准MAS模型检验器对候选策略进行形式化验证。这种"生成-验证"架构利用LLM的引导能力导航大规模组合策略空间，同时保留形式化正确性：只有通过验证器认证的生成策略才会被接受。实例化该框架用于NatATL中的有界战略推理，并构建了首个包含4211个实例的NatATL策略合成数据集。

Result: 使用开源Qwen3-32B模型进行实验，该认证流程在策略合成结果上达到了92%的准确率，证明了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文成功将LLM集成到形式化验证流程中，解决了传统方法计算成本高的问题，为多智能体系统的策略合成提供了一种既高效又保持形式化正确性的新方案。该框架具有通用性，可应用于其他战略逻辑系统。

Abstract: Reasoning about what agents can achieve through strategic interaction is a core challenge in Multi-Agent Systems (MAS). Logics for strategic ability, such as ATL, provide rigorous methods, but their adoption is often hindered by the computational cost of strategy synthesis. We introduce a neuro-symbolic framework that integrates large language models (LLMs) into the model-checking pipeline for MAS. The LLM acts as a strategy-generation oracle, proposing candidate strategies that are then formally validated by a standard MAS model checker. This generate-and-certify architecture uses LLM guidance to navigate large combinatorial strategy spaces while preserving formal soundness: generated strategies are accepted only when certified by the verifier. We instantiate the framework for bounded strategic reasoning in NatATL and introduce the first NatATL strategy-synthesis dataset, consisting of 4211 instances. Experiments with an open-weight Qwen3-32B model show that our certified pipeline achieves 92\% accuracy on strategy-synthesis outcomes.

</details>


### [4] [Intelligence Entropy Principle and the ADE Stability Engineering Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18065)
*Dexing Liu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了智能熵原理来解释LLM多智能体系统在生产环境中的非线性退化，通过构建ADE四层框架和弹性组织形态，将通道断裂率从69-98%降至接近0%，系统死亡概率控制在0.02%以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM驱动的多智能体系统从实验室走向生产环境，系统行为表现出非线性退化现象，需要理论框架来理解和解决系统无序化问题。

Method: 提出智能熵原理（S(t) = S0 * exp(alpha*t/Cm)）并推导稳定条件lambda > alpha/Cm；构建ADE四层框架（包含23个核心组件）和五层无序分类法；提出弹性组织作为新的MAS形态；通过10万级实验和33.6天的生产监控进行验证。

Result: 通道断裂率从69-98%降低至接近0%；系统死亡概率低于0.02%。

Conclusion: 概率驱动的多智能体系统会自发向无序漂移，通过建立四层工程框架和弹性组织形态，可以有效控制系统退化，实现生产级稳定性。

Abstract: As LLM-driven multi-agent systems (MAS) transition from lab to production, system behavior exhibits nonlinear degradation. We introduce the Intelligence Entropy Principle: probability-driven systems spontaneously drift toward disorder, formalized as S(t) = S0 * exp(alpha*t/Cm), where Cm is a model capability coefficient we propose. Lyapunov analysis yields the stabilization condition lambda > alpha/Cm. We construct the ADE (Agent Delivery Engineering) four-layer framework (L1 Physical Laws through L4 User Adaptation) with 23 core components. Validation spans 100K-scale experiments and 33.6 days of production monitoring. We propose a Five-Layer Disorder Taxonomy unifying failures under structural collapse, and present Elastic Organization as an original MAS morphology. Results: channel fracture reduced from 69-98% to near 0%; system death probability below 0.02%.

</details>


### [5] [On the Reliability of Networks of AI Agents: Density Evolution, Stopping Sets, and Architecture Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18121)
*Ehsan Aghazadeh,Hossein Pishro-Nik*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文将多智能体AI系统建模为稀疏图上的消息传递，扩展了LDPC码的密度演化理论，分析了三种失败模式的传播，并证明了预测未解决子问题比例的渐近理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统越来越多地采用多智能体协作的方式解决任务（如提议、验证、组合），这些系统往往优于单一模型，但其成功或失败的原因尚不清楚。作者希望建立一个理论框架来理解和预测这类系统的行为。

Method: 将任务建模为耦合的二元子声明集合，智能体架构建模为稀疏的角色类型化因子图。验证节点是噪声布尔验证器，计算局部布尔函数。考虑三种失败模式（智能体弃权、验证器无输出、消息丢失）作为擦除传播。扩展了LDPC码的密度演化机制，提出了逻辑强制规则，适用于XOR、AND、OR、蕴含和Horn约束。

Result: 证明了密度演化定理，能够预测随机角色类型化架构上未解决子声明的渐近比例。扩展到了确定性的局部树状图序列。XOR情况恢复了经典LDPC在二元擦除信道上的递归；AND情况揭示了正负验证器证书之间的不对称性。由于验证器函数的非线性和价值不对称性，以及三种失败模式不能归约为单一有效信道，需要新的阈值、有限长度和逆结果。

Conclusion: 这不仅仅是LDPC理论的简单重新标记，而是为多智能体AI系统提供了新的理论基础。该框架能够预测系统在随机架构下的渐近性能，并为理解协作AI系统的失败模式提供了理论工具。研究揭示了不同验证器类型（如XOR vs AND）的行为差异，为设计更可靠的协作AI系统提供了指导。

Abstract: Modern AI systems increasingly solve a task not with a single model call but with several imperfect agents working together: some propose pieces of a solution, others verify them, and the results are combined. These systems often outperform any single model, yet it is rarely clear why they succeed or when they will fail. We model such a system as message passing on a sparse graph, the structure that underlies low-density parity-check (LDPC) codes, and extend the density-evolution machinery of coding theory to this richer setting. In our model a task is a set of coupled binary subclaims, and an agent architecture is a sparse, role-typed factor graph whose check nodes are noisy Boolean verifier nodes, each computing a local Boolean function of the subclaims it touches. Three distinct failure modes, all modeled as erasures (an agent abstaining, a verifier returning no usable output, and a message lost between two agents), propagate as the agents exchange set-valued messages. The check agents combine these messages by a single logical-forcing rule that specializes to XOR, AND, OR, implication, and Horn constraints. This is more than a relabeling of LDPC theory: the verifier functions are nonlinear and value-asymmetric, and the three failure modes do not reduce to a single effective channel, so they require new threshold, finite-length, and converse results rather than a direct reuse of parity-check density evolution. We prove a density-evolution theorem that predicts the asymptotic fraction of unresolved subclaims on random role-typed architectures, with an extension to deterministic, locally tree-like graph sequences. The XOR case recovers the classical LDPC recursion on the binary erasure channel (BEC); the AND case exposes an asymmetry between positive and negative verifier certificates.

</details>

### [6] [Surveying GenAI-based Automation in Printed Circuit Board Design and Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17074)
*Sahana Srinivasan,Benjamin Turnbull,Hammond Pearce*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本综述首次系统梳理了生成式人工智能（GenAI）在印刷电路板（PCB）设计全生命周期中的应用，涵盖供应链、系统规范、电路设计、布局优化、验证测试及组装分发等环节，分析了现有工作、技术挑战并展望未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GenAI在硬件领域的研究主要集中于集成电路（IC）设计与硬件描述语言自动化，针对印刷电路板（PCB）这一关键硬件类型的系统性研究缺失。PCB设计流程复杂、涉及环节众多，亟需梳理GenAI在该领域的应用现状与潜力，以填补研究空白。

Method: 对GenAI在PCB设计生命周期各阶段（供应链管理、系统规范制定、电路设计、布局与优化、验证与测试、PCB组装与分发）的应用进行系统性文献调研；构建分类体系，按研究意图与贡献对已有工作进行归类与分析。

Result: 提出了GenAI在PCB设计领域的分类体系，明确了各阶段的研究进展与空白；识别出两大核心技术挑战：领域专用数据稀缺、与现有PCB设计工具的集成支持有限；揭示了GenAI在PCB设计与测试任务中的巨大应用潜力与众多未探索的研究机会。

Conclusion: GenAI在PCB设计领域尚处于早期探索阶段，数据与工具集成是主要瓶颈；未来研究应重点关注领域数据构建、工具链深度集成以及跨阶段协同优化，以充分释放GenAI在PCB设计全流程中的价值。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) is increasingly used for applications in the hardware and software domains. It purports to reduce the manual effort involved in the development and testing of complex systems before release. Within the hardware space, most tasks have focused on design automation of integrated circuits, particularly with hardware description languages. However, other types of hardware also exist! In this survey, we instead examine how GenAI has been and is being across the printed circuit board (PCB) design life cycle. This includes everything from supply chains, system specification, circuit design, layout and optimisation, validation and test, and PCB assembly and distribution. Through this lens we present a taxonomy of discovered works, categorising them according to their intent and contributions. This survey also identifies key technical challenges that GenAI faces in this space, such as domain-specific data scarcity and limited support for integration with existing PCB tools. Finally, future research directions are discussed: our survey shows that there are many opportunities remaining when considering how GenAI may be integrated into various tasks in PCB design and test.

</details>


### [7] [The Price of Anarchy in Disaggregated Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17081)
*Athos Georgiou*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文首次对解耦推理架构进行博弈论分析，揭示了GPU饱和度引起的三个性能 regime，并设计了自适应控制器，在13%吞吐量代价下将 PoA-hat 降低3.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解耦推理架构将 prefill 和 decode 阶段物理分离到不同的 GPU 池，形成了竞争性的"智能体"共享固定的硬件预算。目前缺乏对这种架构的正式博弈论分析，需要理解这些竞争"智能体"之间的博弈行为及其对系统性能的影响。

Method: 1. 将解耦服务建模为三个耦合博弈：prefill 和 decode 池之间的双人资源博弈、层级化 KV 缓存上的自私缓存博弈、具有正外部性的请求路由拥塞博弈。
2. 实证验证后两个博弈，理论分析资源博弈。
3. 表征 GPU 饱和度如何引发 regime 转变，改变博弈的收益结构。
4. 设计自适应控制器实时检测饱和转变并调整路由参数。
5. 在 3 节点 NVIDIA B200 集群上使用 Dynamo 框架，测试 Nemotron-4-340B 和 Llama-3.1-70B 两个模型。

Result: 1. 在饱和以下，自私行为具有有界的 Price of Anarchy (PoA)。
2. 在饱和时，超线性延迟和缓存外部性驱动 PoA-hat 上升。
3. 两个模型都展现出相同的三 regime PoA-hat 结构，第一个膝后网格点都是 C=128。
4. 在 70B 1P/5D 拓扑上，PoA-hat 在饱和阶段从 66.4 降至 21.5（降低 3.1 倍），代价是 13% 的吞吐量。
5. 在 70B 1P/2D 上，PoA-hat 降低 2.2 倍，TTFT P99 降低 7.6 倍。

Conclusion: 自适应路由策略成功将每个模型转移到更好的操作点。博弈论分析为解耦推理架构的设计和优化提供了新的理论视角和实践指导，证明了在饱和 regime 中，通过从缓存亲和性利用转向负载均衡的拥塞避免，可以显著改善系统性能。

Abstract: Disaggregated inference architectures physically separate prefill and decode phases onto distinct GPU pools, creating competing "agents" that share a fixed hardware budget. We provide, to our knowledge, the first formal game-theoretic analysis of this architecture, using NVIDIA Dynamo as a concrete case study. We model disaggregated serving as three coupled games: a two-player resource game between prefill and decode pools, a selfish caching game over the hierarchical KV cache, and a congestion game with positive externalities for request routing. We empirically validate the latter two; the P/D resource game is treated analytically (Section 9.2). We characterize how GPU saturation induces regime transitions that shift the game's payoff structure: below saturation, selfish behavior has bounded Price of Anarchy (PoA); at saturation, superlinear latency and cache externalities drive our empirical estimator PoA-hat (defined in Section 6.4) upward. Based on this analysis, we design an adaptive controller that detects saturation transitions in real time and adjusts routing parameters accordingly, shifting from cache-affinity exploitation to load-balanced congestion avoidance. We instantiate our framework on a 3-node NVIDIA B200 cluster running Dynamo with two models, Nemotron-4-340B (TP=8, full-node workers with cross-InfiniBand KV transfers) and Llama-3.1-70B (TP=4), and find the same three-regime PoA-hat structure with the same first post-knee grid point (C=128) on both models. Adaptive routing shifts each model to a better operating point. Our strongest result is on the 70B 1P/5D topology, where PoA-hat drops 3.1x (66.4 to 21.5) in the saturated phase at a 13% throughput cost. On the 70B 1P/2D, PoA-hat drops 2.2x and TTFT P99 drops 7.6x (see Section 8.5).

</details>


### [8] [Prefill/Decode-Aware Evaluation of LLM Inference on Emerging AI Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17104)
*Shun Usami,Venkatram Vishwanath,E. Wes Bethel*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文对Llama2-7B模型在GPU和新兴AI加速器上的推理性能进行了分阶段（Prefill和Decode）评估。研究发现GPU在计算密集的Prefill阶段表现优异，而GroqRack在Decode阶段实现了更低的TPOT（但不支持批处理）。随着批大小增加，GPU在Decode吞吐量上重新获得优势。文章还分析了异构Prefill/Decode分解方案在不同加速器平台上的性能增益及其适用条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）在延迟敏感和成本敏感场景中的广泛部署，推理效率已成为核心系统挑战。虽然GPU主导当前部署，但越来越多的AI加速器声称在LLM推理中具有优势，然而目前尚不清楚这些加速器在何种条件下能在实践中超越GPU。

Method: 本文提出了一种分阶段感知的LLM推理性能评估方法，在GPU和新兴AI加速器上使用统一的Llama2-7B模型进行测试。通过将执行分解为Prefill和Decode两个阶段，分别测量时间到首个token（TTFT）和每个输出token时间（TPOT）等延迟指标，揭示不同加速器在各阶段的性能特征。

Result: 研究结果表明：1）GPU在计算密集型的Prefill阶段始终表现优异；2）GroqRack在Decode阶段实现了显著更低的TPOT（当前不支持批处理）；3）随着批大小增加，GPU在Decode吞吐量方面重新获得优势；4）每个平台展现出独特的阶段依赖性优势。研究还分析了异构Prefill/Decode分解方案的性能增益，并确定了实现这些增益所需的工作负载和网络条件。

Conclusion: 不同加速器平台在LLM推理的不同阶段展现出各自独特的优势。GPU在Prefill阶段具有稳定优势，而特定加速器（如GroqRack）在Decode阶段可能提供更低的延迟。然而，这些优势受到批处理能力和工作负载特征的影响。异构分解方案可以在特定条件下实现性能增益，为LLM推理系统的优化提供了新的思路。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in latency- and cost-sensitive settings, inference efficiency has become a central systems challenge. While GPUs dominate current deployments, a growing number of AI accelerators claim advantages for LLM inference, yet it remains unclear under which conditions such accelerators outperform GPUs in practice. Recent inference systems decompose execution into Prefill and Decode phases, which exhibit distinct computational characteristics and latency metrics, commonly captured by time to first token (TTFT) and time per output token (TPOT).
  This paper presents a phase-aware evaluation of LLM inference performance across GPUs and emerging AI accelerators using a common model, Llama2-7B. By separately measuring Prefill and Decode performance, we reveal that accelerator advantages differ by phase and metric. Our results show that GPUs consistently excel in the compute-intensive Prefill phase, while GroqRack achieves significantly lower TPOT during Decode (batching not currently supported). However, GPUs regain an advantage in Decode throughput as batch size increases.
  These findings demonstrate that each platform exhibits distinct phase-dependent strengths. We further analyze heterogeneous Prefill/Decode disaggregation across different accelerator platforms, identifying performance gains and the workload and network conditions under which such gains are realized.

</details>


### [9] [Shift-Left High-Level Synthesis Verification via Knowledge-Augmented LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17128)
*Zhihan Xiao,Zhe Zhao,Luke Ztz Hu,Songping Mai*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个知识增强、智能体驱动的HLS移左验证框架，用于在综合前自动检查golden C与HLS-C实现之间的功能一致性，通过双层次一致性检查机制和异构HLS验证知识图谱，在107个HLS基准测试对上实现了98.26%的平均覆盖率和95.33%的动态一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高级综合(HLS)中，golden C规范与HLS导向的C实现之间的功能一致性验证是一项关键但劳动密集型任务。虽然大语言模型(LLM)在自动化测试平台生成方面展现出潜力，但其随机性往往导致覆盖率不足、验证环境不一致以及等价性检查结果不可靠。

Method: 提出一个知识增强、智能体驱动的移左验证框架：1)引入双层次一致性检查机制，联合执行静态结构对齐和动态行为等价性检查；2)集成符号执行和覆盖率驱动的精化来提高验证完整性；3)构建异构HLS验证知识图谱，为测试平台生成提供拓扑感知的推理先验；4)设计自主验证智能体来编排跨异构工具链的迭代精化和故障诊断。

Result: 在107个HLS基准测试对上的实验结果表明，该框架实现了98.26%的平均覆盖率和95.33%的动态一致性，性能优于代表性的基于AST、检索增强和迭代智能体的基线方法。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了LLM在HLS验证中的局限性，通过知识图谱增强和智能体编排实现了高质量的功能一致性验证，为HLS设计流程提供了可靠的自动化验证解决方案。

Abstract: High-Level Synthesis (HLS) enables rapid hardware development by translating C/C++ programs into hardware implementations. Functional consistency verification between golden C specifications and HLS-oriented C implementations is a critical yet labor-intensive task in HLS design flows. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently shown promise in automated testbench generation, their stochastic nature often leads to insufficient coverage, inconsistent verification environments, and unreliable equivalence checking results. To address these limitations, we propose a knowledge-augmented, agent-driven shift-left verification framework for automated functional consistency checking between golden C and HLS-C implementations before synthesis. The framework introduces a Dual-Tier Consistency Checking mechanism that jointly enforces static structural alignment and dynamic behavioral equivalence between paired testbenches, while integrating symbolic execution and coverage-driven refinement to improve verification completeness. Furthermore, we construct a heterogeneous HLS Verification Knowledge Graph to provide topology-aware reasoning priors for testbench generation, and design an autonomous verification agent to orchestrate iterative refinement and failure diagnosis across heterogeneous toolchains. Experimental results on 107 HLS benchmark pairs demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves 98.26\% average coverage and 95.33\% dynamic consistency, outperforming representative AST-based, retrieval-augmented, and iterative agent-based baselines. https://github.com/cz-5f/HLS-LeVeri.git

</details>


### [10] [From Compression to Deployment: Real-Time and Energy-Efficient FastGRNN on Ultra-Constrained Microcontrollers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17249)
*Emre Can Kizilates*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文实现了FastGRNN在资源极度受限的8位Arduino和16位MSP430微控制器上的端到端部署，仅占用566字节权重，在HAPT测试集上达到0.918的macro F1，实现实时推理（Arduino 9.21ms/样本），并通过查找表优化实现96.7%的能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习领域的主流发展趋势是不断增大模型规模、加速器和内存预算，但全球半导体供应限制以及在线推理日益增长的能源和碳排放成本暴露了这一发展路径的脆弱性。因此，本文旨在探索相反方向：重构AI/ML算法以适配已大规模生产的小型、无处不在的微控制器，如可穿戴设备、传感器和边缘设备中的8位和16位微控制器。

Method: 1. **模型压缩流水线**：结合低秩权重分解、迭代硬阈值稀疏化和每张量Q15训练后量化（带显式激活校准）。
2. **端到端部署**：在两个裸机平台上部署——8位Arduino（ATmega328P）和16位MSP430（无硬件乘法器，16KB Flash，512B SRAM）。
3. **查找表优化**：针对无乘法器的MSP430，使用256项sigmoid/tanh查找表，实现30.5倍加速。
4. **跨平台位等效确定性推理**：确保微控制器实现与PyTorch参考实现之间的预测一致性。

Result: 1. **模型效率**：部署模型仅占用566字节权重，在HAPT测试集上达到macro F1 = 0.918（种子0；五种子的Q15平均值为0.853±0.107）。
2. **预测一致性**：在3,399个测试窗口中，与PyTorch参考实现达到100%预测一致（MCU种子0；五种子的C等效性为99.91%-100%）。
3. **实时推理性能**：两个平台均支持实时50Hz流式推理（Arduino每样本9.21ms；MSP430每样本13ms）。
4. **能耗优化**：硬件能耗特性显示17.7mW有源推理功耗，<0.09mW空闲功耗，使用LUT实现96.7%的能耗降低。
5. **循环预热延迟**：中位数74个样本（1.48秒），最坏情况125个样本（2.50秒）。

Conclusion: 本文成功将FastGRNN紧凑门控循环单元部署到资源极度受限的微控制器上，证明了在512B SRAM和16KB Flash的设备上实现高质量实时推理的可行性。研究扩展了原始FastGRNN论文，提供了跨平台位等效确定性推理、循环预热延迟特征化、无乘法器嵌入式目标的可部署查找表方案，以及硬件能耗特征化。这为在边缘设备上部署机器学习模型提供了完整的技术方案和实践指导。

Abstract: The dominant trajectory of modern machine learning has been to scale up: larger models, larger accelerators, larger memory budgets. Yet a multi-year global semiconductor supply constraint and the growing energy and carbon cost of always-online inference expose the fragility of this trajectory and motivate the opposite direction: refactoring AI and ML algorithms to fit the small, ubiquitous microcontrollers already in mass production in wearables, sensors, and edge appliances. We present an end-to-end open-source reproduction of FastGRNN, a compact gated recurrent cell, deployed on two bare-metal targets: the 8-bit Arduino (ATmega328P) and the 16-bit MSP430 (no hardware multiplier; 16 KB Flash; 512 B SRAM). Our compression pipeline combines low-rank weight factorization, iterative hard-thresholding sparsity, and per-tensor Q15 post-training quantization with explicit activation calibration. The deployed model occupies 566 bytes of weights and achieves macro F1 = 0.918 (seed 0; five-seed Q15 mean 0.853+-0.107) on the HAPT test set. It matches a PyTorch reference at 100% prediction agreement across 3,399 test windows (MCU seed 0; 99.91-100% C-equivalent across five seeds). Both platforms sustain real-time 50 Hz streaming inference (9.21 ms per sample on Arduino; 13 ms on MSP430), where a 256-entry sigmoid/tanh look-up table delivers a 30.5x speedup on the multiplier-less MSP430. Four contributions extend the original FastGRNN paper: (i) cross-platform bit-equivalent deterministic inference; (ii) characterization of recurrent warm-up latency (median 74 samples, 1.48 s; worst-case 125 samples, 2.50 s over 100 test windows); (iii) a deployable look-up-table recipe for multiplier-less embedded targets; and (iv) hardware energy characterization showing 17.7 mW active inference power, <0.09 mW idle power, and 96.7% energy reduction with the LUT.

</details>


### [11] [PDAGENT-BENCH: Characterizing, Grounding, and Architecting LLM Agents for VLSI Physical Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17253)
*Qiufeng Li,Rongqian Chen,Quan Cheng,Chengxuan Wang,Sizhe Tang,Wuxi Li,Duo Ding,Chia-Tung Ho,Haoxing Ren,David Z. Pan,Tian Lan,Weidong Cao*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了PDAGENT-BENCH，首个用于评估LLM/VLM在VLSI物理设计中能力的综合基准，包含353个任务和五维能力评估，揭示了现有模型在工具执行和长程推理方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型和视觉语言模型在VLSI前端设计中取得了成功，但在物理设计领域的能力尚未得到充分探索。主要原因是缺乏标准化基准来评估需要在严格设计约束下进行高维多阶段优化、与多种EDA工具协调交互以及迭代优化的智能体物理设计工作流。

Method: 提出PDAGENT-BENCH基准，包含353个精心设计的任务，涵盖概念问题和真实工业场景。评估框架整合任务级评估和工作流级执行，覆盖五个关键能力维度：基础知识、报告理解、根因分析、脚本生成和全流程实现。提供统一的人机对齐智能体物理设计工作流框架，支持在真实EDA环境中进行闭环评估。

Result: 在11个最先进模型上的实验表明：现代LLM/VLM在概念任务上表现良好，但在工具执行方面存在显著局限（如Innovus脚本生成仅42.2%），在长程多阶段推理方面表现不足。研究表明，结合人类技能增强的智能体工作流能显著提升端到端物理设计性能。

Conclusion: PDAGENT-BENCH建立了一个标准化、可重复、真实的评估框架，用于推进LLM/VLM驱动的整体物理设计自动化发展，为未来研究提供了重要基准。基准和框架即将开源。

Abstract: Large Language Models and vision-language models have shown remarkable success in the front-end design of Very Large-Scale Integrated Circuits, yet their capabilities for VLSI physical design remain significantly underexplored. The primary cause is the lack of standardized benchmarks for evaluating agentic physical design workflows that require high-dimensional, multi-stage optimization under strict design constraints, coordinated interaction with diverse Electronic Design Automation tools, and iterative refinement. This work introduces PDAGENT-BENCH, a comprehensive and multi-dimensional benchmark for evaluating LLM/VLM-based agents across the physical design stack. PDAGENT-BENCH integrates both task-level assessment and workflow-level execution. The benchmark suite contains 353 curated problems that combine conceptual questions with real-world industrial artifacts, with expert-validated references and executable solutions. These tasks cover five key capability dimensions: foundational knowledge, report comprehension, root-cause analysis, script generation, and full-flow implementation. In addition, the benchmark provides a unified, human-aligned agentic physical design workflow framework that enables closed-loop evaluation of holistic physical design in realistic EDA environments. Experiments on 11 state-of-the-art models reveal that while modern LLMs/VLMs perform competitively on conceptual tasks, they remain substantially limited in tool-centric execution (e.g., 42.2% on Innovus script generation) and long-horizon, multi-stage reasoning. Our studies further show that human-skill-enhanced agentic workflows significantly improve end-to-end physical design performance. PDAGENT-BENCH establishes a standardized, reproducible, and realistic evaluation framework for advancing LLM/VLM-driven holistic physical design automation. We will open source the benchmark and framework soon.

</details>


### [12] [AUTOGATE: Automated Clock Gating via Toggling-Aware LLM-based RTL Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17461)
*Yiting Wang,Chenhui Deng,Chia-Tung Ho,Yanqing Zhang,Zhuo Feng,Cunxi Yu,Ang Li,Gang Qu,Brucek Khailany*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了AUTOGATE，首个面向工业级RTL功耗优化的智能体框架，通过ML-LLM协同设计解决细粒度时钟门控优化中的长波形处理和大规模代码库扩展难题，在工业设计中实现最高19.34%的动态功耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度时钟门控(FGCG)是降低动态功耗的最有效技术之一，但现有优化流程主要依赖人工操作。基于LLM的RTL优化方法存在两大局限：(1)无法处理跨越数百万周期的长波形轨迹；(2)难以在保持正确性的前提下扩展到大规模层次化代码库的优化。

Method: 提出ML-LLM协同设计框架：设计基于机器学习的聚类算法，将原始翻转轨迹提炼为紧凑的结构化表示，指导LLM进行RTL重写，无需LLM直接处理原始波形数据。采用层次化多智能体架构，将大型设计分解为可独立优化的模块，实现深度设计层次间的协同优化。

Result: 在小规模设计套件上平均降低动态功耗49.31%；在工业级设计上，NVDLA降低19.34%，BlackParrot降低7.96%；在高度优化的专有生产设计上最高降低6.86%。

Conclusion: AUTOGATE成功实现了业界首个面向工业级RTL功耗优化的智能体框架，通过ML-LLM协同和层次化多智能体架构有效解决了波形处理和可扩展性挑战，在各类设计规模上均展现出显著的功耗优化效果。

Abstract: Fine-grain clock gating (FGCG) is among the most effective techniques for reducing dynamic power, yet current FGCG optimization flows remain largely manual. Recent LLM-based RTL optimization approaches remain limited by two key drawbacks: (1) the inability to process long waveform traces spanning millions of cycles, and (2) the difficulty of scaling optimization to large hierarchical codebases while preserving correctness. In this work, we present AUTOGATE, the first agentic framework for industry-grade RTL power optimization, enabling workload-aware clock-gating optimization across large hierarchical codebases. AUTOGATE introduces a Machine Learning (ML)-LLM co-design that bridges waveform-level analysis and RTL rewriting. Specifically, we design an ML-based clustering algorithm that distills raw toggling traces into compact, structured representations that guide LLM-based RTL rewriting. This enables accurate identification and application of clock-gating opportunities without requiring LLMs to directly process raw waveform data. To enhance scalability, AUTOGATE employs a hierarchical multi-agent architecture that decomposes large designs into independently optimizable modules, enabling coordinated optimization across deep design hierarchies. We evaluate AUTOGATE on a diverse set of designs ranging from small RTL designs to large industrial-grade codebases. Experimental results show that AUTOGATE consistently reduces dynamic power relative to baselines. Across the small-design suite, AUTOGATE reduces dynamic power by 49.31% on average. On industry-scale designs, it achieves 19.34% and 7.96% dynamic power reductions on NVDLA and BlackParrot, respectively, and up to 6.86% on highly optimized proprietary production designs.

</details>


### [13] [MIVE: A Minimalist Integer Vector Engine for Softmax LayerNorm and RMSNorm Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17781)
*Kosmas Alexandridis,Giorgos Dimitrakopoulos*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出MIVE，一种统一的极简整数向量引擎架构，通过共享数据通路高效执行LayerNorm、RMSNorm和Softmax三种非线性归一化操作，解决了现有加速器资源重复的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）对专用硬件加速器的延迟和功耗要求日益严格。尽管矩阵乘法是主要计算负载，但LayerNorm、RMSNorm和Softmax等非线性向量归一化操作已成为关键硬件瓶颈。现有加速器通常为这些操作采用专用硬件模块，导致资源重复和硅片利用率低下。

Method: 提出一种极简整数向量引擎（MIVE），这是一种可编程架构，能够通过统一的数据通路执行上述三种操作。该方法利用LayerNorm、RMSNorm和Softmax之间的共同计算模式，最大化硬件共享，同时降低实现开销。

Result: 基于物理ASIC实现的结果表明，MIVE在提供全面多功能支持的同时，在面积效率和硬件效率方面优于大多数最先进的独立加速器。

Conclusion: MIVE架构成功证明了通过识别和利用不同归一化操作间的计算共性，可以在单一可编程数据通路中实现高效的多功能支持，为LLM推理加速器的设计提供了更优的面积和硬件效率方案。

Abstract: The rapid growth of Large Language Models (LLMs) has intensified the need for specialized hardware accelerators that can satisfy stringent inference latency and power constraints. Although matrix multiplications dominate the overall computational workload, non-linear vector normalization operations, such as LayerNorm, RMSNorm and Softmax can become critical hardware bottlenecks. Existing accelerators typically implement these functions using dedicated hardware blocks, leading to duplicated resources and inefficient silicon utilization. To address this limitation, we propose a Minimalist Integer Vector Engine (MIVE), a programmable architecture capable of executing all three operations within a unified datapath. By exploiting common computational patterns across LayerNorm, RMSNorm and Softmax the proposed vector engine maximizes hardware sharing while reducing implementation overhead. Physical ASIC implementation results show that MIVE provides comprehensive multi-function support while achieving higher area and hardware efficiency than most state-of-the-art standalone accelerators.

</details>


### [14] [CUTh-Solver: GPU-Accelerated Sparse Matrix Solver for High-Resolution Thermal Simulation of 3D ICs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17850)
*Chenghan Wang,Zhen Zhuang,Shui Jiang,Siyuan Liang,Xiaoman Yang,Kai Zhu,Darong Huang,Luis Costero,Rongmei Chen,Tsung-Wei Huang,David Atienza,Tsung-Yi Ho*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了CUTh-Solver，一个针对3D IC热仿真中SPD稀疏矩阵系统的GPU加速PCG求解器框架，通过存储压缩、内存访问优化、高并行度预条件策略和自适应混合精度计算，相比GPU加速的COMSOL实现最高25.8倍加速，相比NVIDIA通用库实现超过3倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗粒度热仿真容易低估局部热问题并遗漏关键热点，需要细粒度分析，但这会急剧增加网格分辨率和计算负载。虽然系数矩阵通常稀疏且具有规则稀疏模式，但现有GPU通用矩阵求解器未能利用这些领域特定特性，在数据存储、内存访问、并行性、计算效率和硬件利用率方面遇到瓶颈。

Method: 1) 存储优化：压缩DIA存储格式以消除冗余；2) 内存访问优化：采用对角线方向的SpMV实现合并内存访问；3) 预条件策略：观察到并行性与预条件质量的冲突，采用高并行度预条件策略；4) 计算优化：自适应细粒度混合精度策略，利用多样化浮点单元避免资源争用。

Result: 相比GPU加速的COMSOL Multiphysics 6.4实现最高25.8倍加速；相比NVIDIA原生通用库（AmgX、cuSPARSE、cuDSS）实现超过3倍加速；消融实验验证了每个优化的独立贡献。

Conclusion: 通过针对3D IC热仿真中SPD系统的领域特定特性进行软硬件协同设计，CUTh-Solver在保持数值稳定性的同时显著提升了GPU上的稀疏矩阵求解性能，为高分辨率稳态和瞬态3D IC热仿真提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Coarse-grained thermal simulation tends to underestimate localized thermal issues, potentially missing critical hotspots. Accurate analysis, therefore, demands fine-grained information, which dramatically increases grid resolution and thus computational workload. Fortunately, the coefficient matrices are often sparse with regular sparsity patterns, offering optimization opportunities. However, existing general-purpose matrix solvers on GPUs rarely exploit these domain-specific properties, thereby encountering bottlenecks in data storage, memory access, parallelism, computational efficiency, and hardware utilization. Therefore, we propose CUTh-Solver, a co-designed GPU-accelerated Preconditioned Conjugate Gradient (PCG)-based sparse solver framework for Symmetric Positive Definite (SPD) systems arising from high-resolution steady-state and transient 3D IC thermal simulation. For data storage, CUTh-Solver condenses the Diagonal (DIA) storage format to remove redundancy. To optimize the memory access, CUTh-Solver employs diagonal-wise SpMV to achieve coalesced memory access. We further observe a critical conflict between parallelism and preconditioning quality and thus adopt a high-parallelism preconditioning strategy. To improve computational efficiency and hardware utilization, we employ an adaptive fine-grained mixed-precision strategy that leverages diverse floating-point units to avoid resource contention, enhancing throughput without compromising numerical stability. Experimental results show that CUTh-Solver achieves up to 25.8x speedup over GPU-accelerated COMSOL Multiphysics 6.4 and over 3x speedup over NVIDIA's native general-purpose libraries (AmgX, cuSPARSE, cuDSS). Ablation studies validate the individual contribution of each optimization. The code is available at: https://github.com/Chenghan-Wang/CUTh-Solver

</details>


### [15] [IMPart: Integration of Memetic Operations into Multi-Level Framework for Large-k-Way Hypergraph Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18117)
*Yugao Zhu,Zhicheng Guo,Shang Liu,Mengming Li,Jing Wang,Zhiyao Xie*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出IMPart框架，通过将重组合和变异算子直接集成到多级框架的去粗化阶段，解决了大k值超图划分问题中现有memetic方法计算开销大和可扩展性差的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超图划分方法在大规模划分（大k值）问题中可扩展性不佳，而追求高质量解的memetic方法由于调用独立的多级划分器执行重组合和变异，导致计算时间显著增加，缺乏实用性。

Method: 提出IMPart框架，设计新型重组合和变异算子，将其直接集成到单个多级框架的去粗化阶段，从而将传统多级框架中不同粒度的局部搜索转变为复杂的协作搜索过程。

Result: 在多个标准基准测试上，该方法能更有效地跳出局部最优并在全局解空间中探索，在大k值超图划分问题上显著优于所有现有超图划分器。

Conclusion: IMPart框架为大k值超图划分问题提供了更实用高效的解决方案，展现了超图划分器开发的新范式。

Abstract: The problem of k-way hypergraph partitioning is fundamental with significant applications in various fields, including VLSI design and scientific computing. State-of-the-art hypergraph partitioners commonly employ a multi-level framework encompassing coarsening, initial partitioning, uncoarsening, and refinement phases. However, many existing methods do not scale well to problems requiring a large number of partitions (i.e., large k). In pursuit of exceptionally high solution quality, existing memetic approaches often execute their two key operations, recombination and mutation, by invoking separate, standalone multi-level partitioners. This design choice, however, renders them significantly more time-consuming than standard multi-level partitioners. To make such memetic approaches more practical, we propose an advanced memetic framework, IMPart, which introduces novel recombination and mutation operators and integrates them directly into the uncoarsening phase of a single multi-level framework. This transforms the local searches of different granularities in the traditional multi-level framework into a sophisticated, collaborative search. Experimental results on multiple standard benchmarks demonstrate our framework more effectively escapes local optima and explores the global solution space for higher-quality solutions, substantially outperforming all existing hypergraph partitioners for large-$k$-way hypergraph partitioning. Our framework highlights a new paradigm for the development of advanced hypergraph partitioners.

</details>


### [16] [ComPart: Community-Guided Post-Coarsening for High-Quality Hypergraph Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18131)
*Yugao Zhu,Zhicheng Guo,Yuchao Wu,Mengming Li,Jing Wang,Zhiyao Xie*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了ComPart框架，将社区检测方法集成到超图划分的后粗化阶段（初始划分和反粗化），通过发现的聚类结构指导划分过程以逃离局部最优，并将局部密集分解理论首次推广到超图领域用于指导初始划分，实验证明其解质量优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的超图划分方法主要利用社区检测来约束粗化阶段，但这种范式存在局限性。标准局部细化方法容易陷入局部最优，无法探索全局有意义的解空间。同时，局部密集分解技术此前仅应用于图，尚未推广到超图领域。

Method: 提出ComPart框架，在后粗化阶段（初始划分和反粗化）集成多种社区检测方法，将发现的聚类作为结构指导来改进划分过程；理论上将局部密集分解从图推广到超图领域，提供形式化扩展和必要证明，用于指导初始划分阶段获得更好的起始点。

Result: 在标准基准测试上，该方法在解质量方面持续优于现有最先进方法。框架能够逃离局部最优并探索全局有意义的解子空间，同时灵活兼容现有和未来的社区检测方法。

Conclusion: ComPart框架建立了新的范式，通过在后粗化阶段利用社区结构来克服标准局部细化的局限性，实现了超图划分质量的显著提升。局部密集分解的成功推广填补了该技术在超图领域的应用空白。

Abstract: Hypergraph partitioning is a critical step in the design of complex embedded systems, essential for optimizing task mapping on heterogeneous MPSoCs and enabling multi-FPGA prototyping. Many existing methods rely on community detection to identify modules with dense internal and sparse external connections, typically utilizing them to constrain the coarsening phase--a widely adopted paradigm. In this work, we propose ComPart, a generalized framework that integrates diverse community detection methods to uncover high-quality clusterings throughout the post-coarsening stages (i.e., initial partitioning and uncoarsening). These discovered clusterings serve as distinct structural guides, enabling the refinement process to identify superior partitioning solutions. Our framework offers two key advantages: (1) it establishes a new paradigm that leverages community structures detected during uncoarsening to escape local optima and explore globally meaningful solution subspaces, transcending the limitations of standard local refinements; and (2) it flexibly accommodates both existing and future community detection methods. Furthermore, we theoretically generalize locally-dense decomposition--originally from graphs--to the hypergraph domain. We provide the formal extension and necessary proofs to apply this technique to hypergraphs, marking its first application in hypergraph partitioning. Specifically, we utilize this rigorously derived decomposition to guide the initial partitioning phase toward superior starting points. Experimental results on standard benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in solution quality.

</details>

### [17] [Correct When Paired, Wrong When Split: Decoupling and Editing Modality-Specific Neurons in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17057)
*Tingchao Fu,Wenkai Wang,Fanxiao Li,Huadong Zhang,Jinhong Zhang,Dayang Li,Yunyun Dong,Renyang Liu,Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现多模态大语言模型知识编辑存在"编辑解耦失败"问题，提出DECODE方法通过显式解耦和定位模态特定神经元群来解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型的知识编辑方法存在一个未被充分探索的问题：编辑解耦失败。具体表现为：当模型由多模态输入（文本-图像查询对）触发时，实体相关知识可以被更新；然而，当配对输入被拆分为单模态输入时，模型往往会恢复到编辑前的过时事实。

Method: 提出DECODE方法，通过显式解耦和定位模态特定神经元群来实现针对性的知识更新。该方法基于一个关键发现：MLLMs中的实体知识并非以统一表示形式存储，而是分布在不同模态特定通路中的解耦形式。DECODE能够针对不同模态的电路进行有效更新，解决了偏向多模态查询的更新无法有效传播到单模态电路的问题。

Result: 大量实验表明，DECODE在不同模态触发条件下均能实现有效的知识更新，成功缓解了编辑解耦失败问题。该方法能够在多模态输入和单模态输入情况下都保持知识更新的一致性。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了多模态大语言模型中知识存储的解耦特性，即实体知识分布存储在模态特定通路中，而非统一表示。基于这一发现提出的DECODE方法为解决编辑解耦失败问题提供了有效途径，实现了跨不同模态触发条件的一致性知识更新。

Abstract: Although Knowledge Editing provides an efficient mechanism for updating the knowledge of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), we find that current paradigms still suffer from an important yet remain underexplored issue : editing decoupling failure, where entity-related knowledge can be updated when the model is triggered by multimodal inputs (text--image query pairs), however, it often reverts to outdated pre-edit facts when the paired inputs are split into unimodal ones. Our in-depth empirical analysis reveals that the entity knowledge in MLLMs is not stored as a unified representation, but is instead distributed across disentangled modality-specific pathways. As a result, updates biased toward multimodal queries fail to propagate effectively to unimodal circuits. To bridge this gap, we propose DECODE, which explicitly disentangles and localizes modality-specific neuron groups for targeted knowledge. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DECODE consistently achieves effective knowledge updates under different modality triggers, thereby mitigating editing decoupling failures.

</details>


### [18] [Diagnosing and Repairing Shape-Prior Shortcuts in Long-Range Single-Shot Fringe Projection Profilometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17093)
*Adam Haroon,Anush Lakshman,Cody Fleming,Beiwen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对长距离单次曝光条纹投影轮廓测量(FPP)问题，通过机制可解释性和不确定性量化的诊断发现现有网络依赖物体边界形状先验而非条纹相位解码，提出了架构级修复方案PhiCalNet，将深度回归改为相位输出+可微标定层，使误差降低3.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的单次曝光条纹投影轮廓测量(FPP)主要研究近距离场景，而长距离场景(超过1米)存在三大未解决问题：平方反比强度衰减导致条纹信噪比下降和物理真值退化，单次曝光因缺少条纹阶次信息导致病态问题，以及现有架构缺乏机制性研究。

Method: 采用机制可解释性(MI)和保形不确定性量化(UQ)作为收敛诊断工具，通过线性探测、Grad-CAM和分布外平面测试三种探针定位物理失效点。提出PhiCalNet架构修复方案：输出包裹相位而非深度，应用固定可微标定层将相位映射为深度，从架构而非损失惩罚角度移除形状先验解空间。在包含15,600张条纹图像、50个物体、1.5-2.1米距离的光真实合成基准上验证。

Result: 最佳UNet基线物体平均绝对误差(MAE)为14.54 mm；PhiCalNet将物体MAE降低3.3倍至4.46 mm，残差主要由±π包裹不连续处的0.103%像素承担。拒绝前5%物体像素时，PhiCalNet RMSE降低64%(20.6→7.4 mm)，而基线仅降低3.5%。三种探针收敛确认：基线通过物体边界形状先验而非条纹相位解码求解任务。物理信息损失在深度回归网络上无显著改善，隔离出架构为关键因素。

Conclusion: 机制可解释性和不确定性量化收敛于同一失效位置，成功诊断并验证了架构修复的有效性。PhiCalNet通过架构级别的改进而非软约束惩罚，有效解决了长距离单次曝光FPP中形状先验捷径问题，显著提升了测量精度。

Abstract: Learning-based single-shot fringe projection profilometry (FPP) has been studied mostly at close range. The long-range regime (standoff beyond 1 m) remains largely unaddressed: inverse-square intensity falloff lowers fringe signal-to-noise ratio and degrades physical ground truth, the single-shot problem is ill-posed because fringe-order information is absent from one image, and these architectures have not been studied mechanistically. We present a diagnose-repair-verify study using mechanistic interpretability (MI) and conformal uncertainty quantification (UQ) as convergent diagnostics: they agree on one physical failure locus, driving and verifying an architectural repair. On a photorealistic synthetic benchmark (15,600 fringe images, 50 objects at 1.5-2.1 m), a best UNet baseline reaches 14.54 mm object mean absolute error (MAE). Three probes (linear probing, Grad-CAM, flat-plane out-of-distribution test) converge: the baseline solves the task via object-boundary shape priors rather than fringe-phase decoding. We repair this with PhiCalNet, which outputs wrapped phase rather than depth and applies a fixed differentiable calibration layer mapping phase to depth, removing the shape-prior solution from the hypothesis space architecturally rather than by a loss penalty. A physics-informed loss that enforces the same physics as a soft penalty on a depth-regressing network yields no measurable gain, isolating the architecture as the operative factor. PhiCalNet reduces object MAE 3.3x to 4.46 mm; the residual is carried by 0.103% of pixels at the +/-pi wrap discontinuity. Pixel-wise conformal UQ confirms the diagnosis: rejecting the top 5% of object pixels by snapshot disagreement cuts PhiCalNet RMSE by 64% (20.6->7.4 mm) versus 3.5% for the baseline. MI and UQ converge on the same failure locus.

</details>


### [19] [Informative Missingness to Generate Irregular Clinical Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17106)
*Hadi Mehdizavareh,Gabriele Santangelo,Giovanna Nicora,Simon Lebech Cichosz,Arianna Dagliati,Arijit Khan,Riccardo Bellazzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于扩散模型的临床时间序列生成方法，联合建模实验室检测值及其缺失模式，有效捕捉患者生理状态与临床决策之间的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录中的实验室检测数据收集不规则，检测的缺失本身具有信息价值，反映了临床医生的决策和患者生理状态。现有的方法通常将缺失视为预处理的人工产物，而忽略其临床意义，因此需要直接对缺失模式进行建模，特别是在缺失非随机（MNAR）的情况下。

Method: 基于DACMI基准数据集（源自MIMIC-III），将图表时间对齐到4小时间隔，并将入院记录分割为7天窗口，生成包含实验室检测值和对应观测指标的轨迹。扩展TimeDiff框架，通过互补的扩散目标同时学习连续的实验室检测值和离散的缺失模式。

Result: 实验表明，生成的数据在个体实验室分布和联合值-缺失嵌入方面与真实患者轨迹高度匹配，证明扩散模型能够捕捉MNAR缺失模式下患者生理与临床检测行为之间具有临床意义的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 该方法能够生成保留关键生理-缺失关系的合成先验数据，可作为临床基础模型的初始组件，为后续训练能够利用信息性缺失的先验数据拟合网络奠定基础。

Abstract: Laboratory tests in electronic health records are collected irregularly, and the absence of a test order can be as informative as the measurement itself. Such missingness reflects clinicians' decisions and patient physiology, making it important to model it directly rather than treat it as a preprocessing artifact. Here we present a diffusion-based approach for generating clinical time series that jointly models laboratory values and their observation patterns using the public Data Analytics Challenge on Missing Data Imputation (DACMI) benchmark derived from MIMIC-III. To preserve realistic sampling, we align chart times into 4-hour intervals and segment admissions into 7-day windows, producing trajectories that pair each lab value with a corresponding observation indicator. Standard transformations and normalization are applied to stabilize training. Our method extends the TimeDiff framework to learn continuous lab values and discrete missingness patterns through complementary diffusion objectives. Experiments show that the generated data closely match real patient trajectories across individual lab distributions and joint value-missingness embeddings, demonstrating that diffusion models can capture clinically meaningful dependencies between patient physiology and clinicians' testing behavior under MNAR-like (missing-not-at-random) missingness. These preliminary results indicate that our model can serve as an initial component toward developing clinical foundation models. By producing synthetic priors that preserve key physiology-missingness relationships, this work motivates the subsequent training of Prior-Data Fitted Networks capable of leveraging informative missingness, which we will investigate in the extended work.

</details>


### [20] [Models Take Notes at Prefill: KV Cache Can Be Editable and Composable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17107)
*Bojie Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLM的KV缓存可视为“结论备忘录”，支持直接编辑和跨上下文拼接，在保持决策一致性的同时大幅降低延迟（最高14.9倍），并可与传统前缀缓存兼容，TTT削减53-398倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统前缀缓存要求前缀完全匹配，单字段变更即导致整个下游缓存失效。作者旨在探索是否存在一种机制，能在字段变更时仅覆盖该字段的KV向量而复用其余缓存，从而提升缓存利用率和推理效率。

Method: 通过因果干预实验分析四个模型家族，发现预填充阶段模型已将字段条件化的结论写入下游位置的字段仅贡献不足1%的决策。基于此，提出将KV缓存解读为“结论备忘录”，实现两项能力：(1)可编辑性——对字段进行勘误修正；(2)可组合性——将预编译技能通过RoPE重定位拼接至任意上下文。方法支持多种注意力变体，并通过仅追加勘误与现有前缀缓存无缝集成。

Result: 在8B模型上，结合思维链时仅编辑字段即可恢复决策（计算量约1%），无CoT时编辑被忽略；拼接操作与完全重计算在logit余弦相似度上达0.90-0.999（12个模型），时间复杂度从O(L²)降至O(L)；统一编辑+拼接代理在决策一致的前提下延迟降低高达14.9倍；在线vLLM基准测试中保持98.5%前缀缓存命中率，p90 TTT削减53-398倍。

Conclusion: KV缓存可作为可编辑、可组合的“结论备忘录”使用，该方法适用于任意逐token注意力KV缓存，经跨规模、量化、MoE及多模态缓存验证，并可扩展至多种注意力变体。通过仅追加勘误实现与生产前缀缓存的兼容，为高效KV缓存复用提供了通用解决方案。

Abstract: Prefix caching reuses prefill only across an exactly shared prefix, so one changed field invalidates the entire downstream cache. Yet overwriting the field's own key/value vectors and reusing the rest leaves the model acting on the old value. The reason, established causally across four model families: at prefill the model has already written the field-conditioned conclusion onto downstream notes; the field's own key/value drives under 1% of the decision. Read as a notebook of memoized conclusions, two capabilities follow. (1) It is editable. A salient erratum amends the notes; and with chain-of-thought, editing the field alone recovers the decision (1.00 at 8B, ~1% compute), while without CoT it is ignored. (2) It is composable. The notes are position-portable, so a precompiled skill can be RoPE-repositioned and spliced into any context, indistinguishable from full recompute (logit cosine 0.90-0.999, twelve models) at O(L) rather than O(L^2) time-to-first-token. A unified edit+compose agent stays decision-identical to recompute at up to 14.9x lower latency. The approach applies to any per-token attention KV cache, validated across scale, quantization, Mixture-of-Experts, and multimodal caches, and extends to several attention variants through small adapters. Because the erratum is append-only, it composes with production prefix caching: in an online vLLM benchmark it keeps the prefix cache-aligned (98.5% hit-rate), cutting p90 time-to-first-token by 53-398x.

</details>


### [21] [The Critical Role of Model Selection in Causal Inference: A Comparative Analysis of Classification Models within the InferBERT Framework for Pharmacovigilance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17113)
*Csaba Kiss,Roland Molontay,Gabriele Pergola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在InferBERT框架中，经过领域预训练的BioBERT模型在药物不良反应因果检测任务上表现最佳，超越了简单基线模型和大型医学LLM，证明了领域预训练比模型规模扩展更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: InferBERT框架结合了Transformer模型与Do-calculus进行药物不良反应因果推断，但其性能高度依赖于底层分类模型的选择。目前尚不清楚是否简单模型就足够、领域预训练是否有帮助、以及扩展到大型语言模型是否能提升因果检测性能。

Method: 在两个基准数据集（镇痛药诱导的急性肝衰竭AILF和曲马多相关死亡TRAM）上进行比较研究。评估四种模型：XGBoost（基线）、ALBERT（原始InferBERT）、BioBERT（生物医学Transformer）、Med-LLaMA（医学LLM）。使用5折交叉验证重复20次，测量准确率、校准误差（ECE）以及与三种传统药物警戒信号（PRR、ROR、EBGM）的Jaccard一致性，通过配对t检验验证显著性。

Result: BioBERT在两个数据集上均达到最高准确率，而Med-LLaMA尽管参数量更大且采用参数高效微调，表现却不如预期。领域特定预训练是关键因素。校准改善了ECE但对准确率和因果发现的影响不一。BioBERT的优越性能也产生了与药物警戒信号最强的Jaccard一致性。

Conclusion: 领域特定预训练相比简单基线和大型LLM具有明显优势。对于计算药物警戒，投资于可控的、领域感知的模型比单纯扩大模型规模更有效。这一发现为资源分配提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Distinguishing causal adverse drug events (ADEs) from spurious correlations remains a central challenge in pharmacovigilance. The InferBERT framework integrates transformer models with Do-calculus, but its success hinges on the underlying classification model. This study evaluates the impact of model choice in InferBERT, assessing whether simpler models suffice, if domain-specific pre-training helps, whether scaling to LLMs improves causal detection, and the effect of post-hoc calibration. We performed a comparative study on two benchmarks: Analgesics-induced Acute Liver Failure (AILF) and Tramadol-related Mortalities (TRAM). Four models were evaluated-XGBoost (baseline), ALBERT (original InferBERT), BioBERT (biomedical transformer), and Med-LLaMA (medical LLM)-using 5-fold cross-validation repeated over 20 runs. We measured accuracy, Expected Calibration Error (ECE) pre- and post-isotonic regression, and Jaccard concordance of causal terms with PRR, ROR, and EBGM; significance was tested with paired t-tests. BioBERT achieved the highest accuracy on both datasets, while Med-LLaMA underperformed despite its size and parameter-efficient fine-tuning. Domain-specific pre-training was decisive. Calibration improved ECE but had mixed effects on accuracy and causal discovery. BioBERT's superiority also yielded the strongest concordance with traditional pharmacovigilance signals. These results show that domain-specific pre-training provides a clear advantage over simpler baselines and larger LLMs. Investing in manageable, domain-aware models is more effective for computational pharmacovigilance than simply scaling model size.

</details>

Conclusion: 贡献在于验证过的检测器、精化和可实现性产物；现象和格结构是经典的。这是首个机器检查的此类运行时一致性层级，为多智能体LLM系统提供了形式化的并发控制基础，已在实际系统中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems share state through memory stores, vector indices, and tool registries. We model such sharing as long-running read-generate-write operations under deterministic-generation semantics -- the regime durable-execution engines enforce by deterministic replay -- and formalize four concurrency anomalies in TLA+: stale-generation, phantom-tool, causal-cascade, and tool-effect reordering, structural analogues of classical isolation anomalies, each with a TLC counter-example. The exclusion lattice over these anomalies is trivial; the contribution is the mechanically verified realizability and strict separation of one maximal chain within it, $L_0 \subsetneq \cdots \subsetneq L_4$, to our knowledge the first machine-checked consistency hierarchy for such runtimes. A development of 274 Verus obligations (zero assume, zero admit; trust base: two structural axioms and a mutex correspondence) proves the detectors sound and complete against the specifications and each runtime its avoidance set. Three deployed Rust runtimes realize L0-L1 (pessimistic locking, serializable snapshot isolation, default-SI), each verified against stale-generation and refined to its state machine; L2-L4 are exec-mode-verified with dependency-free prevention twins (A3, A6, A2: 0/1000 versus 1000/1000), and L2 is run live across three model families (A3 prevented in all 120 retracted sessions). We reproduce a silent lost update in ByteDance's deer-flow, formalizing its fix as a verified $L_0 \to L_1$ refinement, and exhibit tool-effect reordering in LangGraph's ToolNode on unmodified output, removed by an L3 commit-order sequencer. The verified detector, refinements, and realizability artifacts are the contribution; the phenomena and lattice are classical.

</details>

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) have emerged as powerful representation extractors for medical data, yet their generalizability to datasets under distribution shift remains underexplored. This work systematically evaluates FM-based representations on a suite of computational pathology tasks across two real-world commercial cohorts, IH-BC and IH-NSCLC, drawn from the licensed in-house (IH) oncology dataset. The analysis focuses on two modalities, whole-slide images and transcriptomic profiles, drawn from the IH multimodal data. We first benchmark unimodal probing performance across five FMs on eight downstream classification tasks, and find that image and omics representations carry complementary predictive signals. Then we investigate whether multimodal fusion can yield additional gains over unimodal baselines by comparing three image-omics fusion strategies built on paired representations. The trustworthiness of selected unimodal and multimodal pipelines is further assessed through conformal prediction. Our results show that FM representations achieve competitive performance on out-of-distribution data and that multimodal fusion helps mainly when no single modality dominates the signal. Conformal prediction reveals that in the majority of cases where a point prediction fails, the true diagnosis remains recoverable within the prediction set, reinforcing the value of uncertainty-aware inference for clinical support.

</details>

*Yuanteng Chen,Peisong Wang,Zhilei Liu,Nanxin Zeng,Yuantian Shao,Shiqiang Lang,Tao Liu,Chuangyi Li,Qinghao Hu,Gang Li,Jing Liu,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对混合专家多模态大语言模型（MoE-MLLMs），提出MODE框架，通过模态解耦的专家级混合精度量化方法，有效解决了视觉token主导导致的专家重要性估计偏差问题，在W3A16下平均性能损失控制在2.9%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE-MLLMs虽然性能优异但GPU内存成本过高，现有专家级混合精度量化方法在MoE-MLLMs上性能下降明显，主要因为专家重要性估计存在两个被忽视的偏差：(1)跨模态层面，视觉token的数量优势使专家选择频率被视觉token主导，掩盖了对文本模态关键的专家；(2)视觉内部层面，大量冗余视觉token扭曲频率统计，掩盖了对信息性视觉内容关键的专家。

Method: 提出MODE（模态解耦专家级混合精度量化）框架：(1)按模态解耦专家选择频率；(2)过滤冗余视觉token获得去噪视觉频率；(3)评估各模态量化敏感性作为频率估计的补充信号；(4)将以上信号整合到整数线性规划(ILP)公式中，在给定预算下分配每专家位宽。

Result: 在MoE-MLLMs上进行了广泛实验，MODE在W3A16设置下平均性能损失限制在2.9%以内，在极端2-bit设置下获得更大提升，证明了该方法特别适用于MoE-MLLMs。

Conclusion: MODE通过模态解耦有效解决了MoE-MLLMs量化中的专家重要性估计偏差问题，实现了性能与压缩率的良好平衡，为MoE-MLLMs的实际部署提供了有效的压缩解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts Multimodal Large Language Models (MoE-MLLMs) offer remarkable performance but incur prohibitive GPU memory costs, making compression essential. Among PTQ methods, expert-level mixed-precision quantization has proven effective for MoE-LLMs, yet suffers notable degradation on MoE-MLLMs due to two overlooked biases in expert importance estimation. (1) At the cross-modal level, the numerical dominance of vision tokens causes expert selection frequency to be dominated by vision tokens, masking experts that are critical to the text modality; (2) at the intra-vision level, the large proportion of redundant vision tokens further skew frequency statistics, obscuring experts critical for informative visual content. To bridge gaps, we propose MODE, a modality-decomposed expert-level mixed-precision quantization framework for MoE-MLLMs that decomposes expert selection frequency by modality, filters redundant vision tokens to obtain denoised visual frequency, and further evaluates quantization sensitivity per modality as a complementary signal to frequency-based estimation. These signals are integrated into an Integer Linear Programming formulation to assign per-expert bit-widths under a given budget. Extensive experiments show that MODE is particularly well-suited for MoE-MLLMs, limiting average performance loss to within 2.9% at W3A16, with larger gains at the extreme 2-bit setting.

</details>


### [25] [Noise-Driven Escape from Metastable Phases explains Grokking in Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17120)
*Ibrahim Talha Ersoy,Karoline Wiesner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过L2正则化强度变化引起的一阶相变和滞后效应，为深度神经网络中“grokking”（突然延迟泛化）现象提供了理论解释，证明其源于模型在亚稳态间的势垒逃逸过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络展现出强大的泛化能力，但存在一个未解之谜——grokking现象：模型在长时间表现出过拟合状态后，突然出现泛化能力的急剧提升。理解这一延迟泛化现象的物理机制对于揭示神经网络学习原理和设计更高效的学习算法具有重要意义。

Method: 1. 基于线性深度神经网络进行分析，利用L2正则化强度作为控制参数研究一阶相变行为；2. 通过L2正则化设计可控的势阱，将模型困在亚稳态中；3. 分析SGD噪声驱动模型跨越能量势垒的逃逸过程，验证逃逸时间服从Arrhenius标度律；4. 使用稀疏子采样技术重现典型grokking曲线；5. 探索该方法在非线性DNN中的适用性。

Result: 1. 证明grokking与L2一阶相变中的滞后效应一致；2. 模型在低精度亚稳态中的逃逸时间跨越两个数量级，服从Arrhenius标度；3. 亚稳态数量等于可学习特征数量——每个数据协方差奇异值对应一个；4. 任务复杂度增加会导致更显著的滞后效应；5. 该机制在一般非线性DNN中同样适用。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了grokking现象的物理本质：通过L2正则化工程化的势阱，模型被困在亚稳态中，SGD噪声驱动其跨越能量势垒实现逃逸，导致延迟泛化。这一发现为设计更高效的学习方案提供了新思路，表明可以通过调控正则化强度和噪声水平来优化学习过程。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) exhibit first order phase transitions under variations of the L2 regularization strength, with each transition marking the onset of a new learnable feature. Below a critical regularization strength, all features are in principle learnable, but coexisting metastable states, separated by energy barriers, can trap the network and impede convergence. A strength of DNNs is their ability to generalize. But many open questions remain, among them the origin of so called grokking: the abrupt, delayed onset of generalization after prolonged apparent overfitting. We show for linear DNNs that grokking is consistent with hysteresis in first-order L2 phase transitions: using L2 regularization to engineer deliberate trapping, we demonstrate that a model in a low-accuracy metastable state escapes only when SGD noise drives it across an energy barrier, with escape times following Arrhenius scaling. We reproduce grokking-like delayed convergence across two orders of magnitude in escape time by deliberately trapping models in metastable phases. Using sparse sub-sampling we also reproduce the canonical grokking curve where test error eventually approaches the final training error. Our work suggests that the number of metastable states equals the number of learnable features -- one per singular value of the data covariance -- the potential for hysteresis grows naturally with task complexity. We provide evidence that the same mechanism likely operates in general nonlinear DNNs. Our results provide routes toward more efficient learning schemes.

</details>


### [26] [Towards Fast GNN Surrogates for CO2 Migration in Complex Geological Formations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17180)
*Rodrigo S. Luna,Thiago H. N. Coelho,Luiz S. L. Neto,Roberto M. Velho,Adriano M. A. Cortes,Renato N. Elias,Alexandre G. Evsukoff,Fernando A. Rochinha,Mauricio Araya-Polo,Herve Gross,Alvaro L. G. A. Coutinho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的图神经网络代理模型，用于预测地质封存中的CO₂羽流迁移，在SPE11A基准测试中实现了对气体饱和度和液相密度的准确预报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多相流在复杂地质构造中的物理行为模拟计算成本高昂，而地质CO₂封存需要快速预测CO₂羽流迁移以进行有效监测。传统数值模拟方法难以满足实时预测需求，因此需要开发高效的数据驱动方法来复现多相流的关键物理特征。

Method: 将SPE11A基准问题重构为图结构，节点表示计算单元，边编码基于传导率的相互作用并融入几何属性。提出各向异性消息传递机制，通过几何条件化的边嵌入计算交互权重，捕捉由网格几何、渗透率差异和地质非均质性引起的方向性输运。采用自回归残差公式在潜在空间建模时间演化，并通过多步监督进行训练。

Result: 在SPE11A基准测试中，该方法能够准确预测气体饱和度和液相密度这两个CO₂封存监测的关键指标。模型有效捕捉了尖锐气水界面、强对流输运和快速对流混合伴随指进发展等关键物理特征。在扩展的预测时间范围内，累积误差保持在适度水平。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据驱动图神经代理模型成功复现了复杂地质构造中多相流的关键物理行为，为CO₂地质封存的监测和预测提供了有效的替代工具，证明了机器学习方法在多相流模拟中的可行性和竞争力。

Abstract: This chapter discusses how a data-driven machine learning approach can reproduce key aspects of the physical behavior of multiphase flows in complex geological formations. We propose an end-to-end graph neural surrogate tailored to CO$_2$ plume migration forecasting in geological storage. The method is evaluated on the SPE11A benchmark, a well-known industry test case designed to assess CO$_2$ storage scenarios and characterized by sharp gas-water interfaces, strong advective transport, and rapid convective mixing with fingering development. The benchmark is reformulated as a graph in which nodes represent computational cells and edges encode transmissibility-based interactions enriched with geometric attributes. Directional transport arising from grid geometry, permeability contrasts, and geological heterogeneity is captured through an anisotropic message-passing mechanism, where interaction weights are computed via geometry-conditioned edge embeddings, biasing message aggregation toward physically relevant transport directions. Temporal evolution is modeled in latent space using an autoregressive residual formulation trained with multi-step supervision. The proposed model produces competitive forecasts of gas saturation and liquid-phase density, which are key indicators for CO$_2$ storage monitoring, with cumulative errors that remain moderate over extended forecasting horizons.

</details>


### [27] [Finsler Geometry, Graph Neural Networks, and You](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17185)
*T. Mitchell Roddenberry,Richard G. Baraniuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于Finsler拉普拉斯算子的新型图神经网络架构，突破了传统方法仅限于各向同性算子的限制，并能收敛到流形上的真实算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于图拉普拉斯算子的图神经网络架构通过逼近Laplace-Beltrami算子进行构建，这限制了其应用仅限于各向同性算子。为了突破这一限制，作者寻求一种非线性的替代方案。

Method: 作者考虑了从流形采样的点云上对Finsler拉普拉斯算子的估计，证明了这些离散估计随着样本点数量的增加而收敛到流形上的真实算子。同时，将此算子表示为图神经网络层，从而定义了一族受Finsler几何约束的Finslerian图神经网络。

Result: 所提出的Finslerian图神经网络能够恢复非线性扩散方程背后的几何结构，并在实际应用中得到了验证。

Conclusion: 该研究成功地将Finsler几何引入图神经网络，通过理论证明和实验验证，展示了其在处理非线性几何问题上的有效性和优越性，为图神经网络的发展提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Graph neural network architectures based on the graph Laplacian approximate the Laplace-Beltrami operator, thus limiting their application to isotropic operators. As a nonlinear alternative to the Laplace-Beltrami operator, we consider estimates of the Finsler Laplacian on point clouds sampled from a manifold. We prove that these discrete estimates converge to the true operator on the manifold as the number of point samples grows. Moreover, we show that this operator can be expressed as a graph neural network layer, which we use to define a family of Finslerian graph neural networks constrained to express Finsler geometry. We show that Finslerian graph neural networks recover the geometry underlying nonlinear diffusion equations in practice.

</details>


### [28] [Reconfigurable Computing Challenge: Transformer for Jet Tagging on Versal AI Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17500)
*Gram Koski,Sean Lipps,Zhenghua Ma,G. Abarajithan,Ryan Kastner*

Main category: cs.LG

Method: 在AMD Versal AI引擎（AIE）上实现了量化整型Transformer，将稠密层和多头注意力（MHA）层映射到AIE tiles。开发了可复用的软件框架，将Transformer层表示为可组合的AIE构建块，并能从高级Python模型描述自动生成相应的Vitis图代码。

Conclusion: 该工作为在资源受限的FPGA加速器上部署Transformer模型提供了基础框架，为未来在粒子物理实验触发器系统中应用深度学习模型开辟了道路。开源发布促进了该领域的进一步研究和开发。

Abstract: Transformer-based models achieve strong performance for jet tagging at the CERN LHC, but deploying them in low-latency, resource-constrained trigger systems is challenging. We present an initial implementation of a quantized, integer-only transformer for jet tagging on the AMD Versal AI Engine (AIE), mapping dense and multi-head attention (MHA) layers to AIE tiles. The main contribution is a reusable software framework that represents transformer layers as composable AIE building blocks and automatically generates the corresponding Vitis graph code from a high-level Python model description. This framework provides a foundation for future research and is released as open-source software at https://github.com/KastnerRG/particle_transformer_aie.

</details>


### [29] [Constrained Diffusion Models with Primal-Dual Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17192)
*Samar Hadou,Yigit Berkay Uslu,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了带有原始-对偶推断(PDI)的约束扩散模型，用于从具有平均约束的熵正则化优化问题的最优分布中采样，通过联合推断最优原始分布和对偶变量，实现了约束条件下的高效采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的约束采样方法通常在采样前估计对偶变量并在生成过程中保持不变，这种静态方法无法准确满足约束条件。本文旨在开发一种动态推断框架，在扩散采样过程中联合优化原始分布和对偶变量，从而更准确地满足平均约束条件。

Method: 在拉格朗日对偶域中形式化约束采样问题，最优分布呈以最优对偶变量为索引的Gibbs分布形式。PDI算法在每个逆向扩散步骤中：(1)使用当前对偶乘数对应的得分场进行去噪；(2)根据去噪样本的约束违背程度通过对偶上升更新乘数。训练单一的对偶条件得分网络，覆盖推理过程中遇到的对偶变量族所诱导的Gibbs分布族。

Result: 证明了推理轨迹上对偶变量的时间平均收敛到对偶最优值的邻域，并通过依赖调度的稳定性因子界定了残差对偶失配对终端分布的影响。在混合高斯分布约束采样、无线资源分配和投资组合管理等任务上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: PDI框架成功实现了约束采样中原始分布和对偶变量的联合推断，理论分析保证了对偶变量的收敛性和分布误差的有界性，实验验证了该方法在多个实际问题上的优越性能，为约束优化问题提供了一个新的有效采样解决方案。

Abstract: This paper develops constrained diffusion models with primal-dual inference (PDI) to sample from optimal distributions of entropy-regularized optimization problems with \emph{average} constraints. We formalize constrained sampling in the Lagrangian dual domain, where the optimal distribution takes the form of a Gibbs distribution indexed by the optimal dual variable. Rather than estimating this dual multiplier before sampling and freezing it throughout generation, PDI jointly infers the optimal primal distribution and its parametrizing dual variable. Each reverse diffusion step denoises using the score field associated with the current multiplier and then updates the multiplier through dual ascent using the estimated constraint violation of the denoised samples. To enable this conditional score field, we train a single dual-conditioned score network over the family of Gibbs distributions induced by the dual variables encountered during inference. We prove that the time average of the dual variables generated along the inference trajectory converges to a neighborhood of the dual optimum and bound the effect of residual dual mismatch on the terminal distribution through schedule-dependent stability factors. We evaluate PDI on constrained sampling from a mixture of Gaussians, wireless resource allocation, and portfolio management.

</details>

Abstract: Embedded machine learning moves inference from cloud services to resource-constrained devices that must acquire data, preprocess signals, run a model, and act within tight limits on memory, energy, and latency. The emphasis is placed on engineering decisions that are often hidden in generic machine-learning introductions: sampling and buffering, feature extraction as dimensionality reduction, validation under class imbalance, model/runtime co-design, and streaming deployment. Two representative signal families are used throughout the paper. The first is inertial motion recognition, where a two-second, three-axis accelerometer window is transformed from raw samples into root-mean-square and spectral features before classification. The second is keyword spotting, where audio is sampled, anti-aliased, transformed into mel-frequency cepstral coefficients, and processed by a compact one-dimensional convolutional network. The paper concludes with practical design rules for robust on-device inference, including data curation, quantization, thresholding, scheduling, and field monitoring.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准在策略蒸馏（OPD）使用学生采样token估计反向KL目标，虽然避免了全词汇表计算，但在实践中存在严重的训练病理问题：样本效率低、生成动态不稳定、与精确全词汇表OPD存在显著性能差距。诊断发现这些问题源于对数比率奖励（log-ratio reward）在构造上无界，导致早期位置产生极高方差梯度并持续整个训练过程。

Method: 提出PowerOPD方法，基于Box-Cox幂变换构建一族原生有界、符号一致的奖励函数，参数化由alpha > 0控制，其中对数比率是alpha -> 0的退化极限。该方法通过alpha参数控制奖励的有界性，从源头解决高方差梯度问题。

Result: 在6个数学推理基准和4个Qwen3师生对上验证：相比原始OPD提升+6.37/+5.71（Avg@8/Pass@8），相比后验稳定化提升+3.01/+3.54，相比全词汇表OPD提升+2.59/+8.90；同时减少59.2%的训练时间和23.1%的GPU峰值内存；梯度范数比原始OPD小3000倍以上；更大的alpha通常提升准确率并缩短响应长度。

Conclusion: PowerOPD通过原生有界的奖励设计有效解决了标准OPD的训练病理问题，在多个基准上显著提升性能，同时大幅降低计算成本。该方法为大语言模型的在策略蒸馏提供了一个更稳定、高效的训练框架。

Abstract: Standard on-policy distillation (OPD) for large language models estimates the reverse-KL objective using student-sampled tokens, yielding an unbiased single-sample Monte Carlo estimator that avoids vocabulary-wide computation. However, we show that this estimator suffers from severe training pathologies in practice: sample inefficiency, unstable generation dynamics, and a substantial performance gap compared to exact full-vocabulary OPD. Reward-level diagnosis traces these pathologies to the log-ratio reward, which is unbounded by construction, producing extremely high-variance gradients concentrated at early positions and persisting throughout training; standard post-hoc scaling fail as they operate only after this distortion occurs. To solve this problem, we propose PowerOPD: a family of natively bounded, sign-consistent rewards from the Box-Cox power transformation, parameterized by alpha > 0, of which the log-ratio is the degenerate alpha -> 0 limit. Across six mathematical reasoning benchmarks and four Qwen3 teacher-student pairs, PowerOPD achieves benchmark-averaged Avg@8/Pass@8 gains of up to +6.37/+5.71 over vanilla OPD, +3.01/+3.54 over post-hoc stabilization, and +2.59/+8.90 over full-vocabulary OPD, while reducing wall-clock time by 59.2% and peak GPU memory by 23.1%. Larger alpha generally improves accuracy, consistently shortens responses, and keeps gradient norms more than 3,000x smaller than vanilla OPD.

</details>

*Xiaoyu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了有界度数证书在离群点检测中的"盲区"精确大小为干净边缘分布的Christoffel函数，并据此提出了重加权铰链方法的分辨率原理，证明了在恶意噪声下鲁棒学习γ-边界半空间的边界-度数权衡和算法性能界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于低阶矩的离群点检测证书存在盲区：攻击者可以将腐败数据隐藏在干净数据本身看起来典型的位置，而这些位置是有界度数测试无法分辨的。作者想要理解这个盲区的精确大小，以及证书方法的局限性。

Method: 1. 提出分辨率原理：度数-2t证书在中心c处能隐藏的最大腐败质量恰好是干净边缘分布的Christoffel函数λ_{t+1}(c)；
2. 基于此原理发展重加权铰链方法来鲁棒学习γ-边界半空间；
3. 使用加权Chebyshev归约技术分析边界-度数权衡。

Result: 1. 边界-度数权衡：认证密集薄饼到误差ε需要SoS度数Ω(log(1/ε))或边界Ω(√log(1/ε)/√d)，解释了Shen(2025)中log(1/ε)边界的必然性；
2. 度数-2离群点障碍：在显式实例上，度数2被困在η^{1/2}而度数4可以突破；
3. 度数-2t算法达到前沿η^{1-1/2t}（在t=1时恢复Shen(2025)的结果），其增益是显式常数且不可改进。

Conclusion: 本文将Christoffel函数的逆解释为有界度数证书无法移除的腐败量，这一分辨率原理揭示了证书方法（而非信息论）的基本限制：离群点检测的分辨率受限于证书的Sum-of-Squares度数，度数-2方法在小崩溃率上的局限源于方法本身而非分析，提供了边界-度数权衡的紧下界。

Abstract: A certificate that removes outliers sees the data only through its low-degree moments, and an adversary exploits exactly this, hiding corruption where the clean data already looks typical, in the blind spot no bounded-degree test resolves. That blind spot turns out to have an exact size: the Christoffel function of the clean marginal, the very quantity modern data analysis thresholds to detect outliers, here read from the adversary's side as the corruption a bounded-degree certificate cannot remove. We turn this inversion into the organizing principle of the reweighted-hinge approach to robustly learning $γ$-margin halfspaces under malicious noise (Shen, 2025; Zeng and Shen, 2025): the governing resource is the Sum-of-Squares degree of the outlier-removal certificate, and the resolution principle states that the maximal corruption mass which can hide at a center $c$ from a degree-$2t$ certificate is exactly the Christoffel function $λ_{t+1}(c)$ of the clean marginal. Three consequences follow, all against the certificate method (not information-theoretic). A margin-degree tradeoff: certifying the dense pancake to error $ε$ costs SoS degree $Ω(\log(1/ε))$ or margin $Ω(\sqrt{\log(1/ε)}/\sqrt{d})$, explaining why the $\log(1/ε)$ margin Shen (2025) records is forced, with a weighted-Chebyshev reduction making the threshold $2t=Θ((|c|/s)^2)$ tight modulo one classical weighted-extremal estimate. A degree-$2$ outlier barrier: the resolution principle realized as an explicit instance on which degree $2$ is stuck at $η^{1/2}$ while degree $4$ escapes, locating the method's small breakdown rate in the degree, not the analysis. And a degree-$2t$ algorithm tracing the frontier $η^{1-1/2t}$ (recovering Shen (2025) at $t=1$), whose gain is an explicit constant, capped by the pancake density and shown unimprovable by the degree-$2$ barrier.

</details>

*Kaviru Hapuarachchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用并发程序的非确定性作为训练信号，通过聚合多次运行的观测事件生成经验分布，对7B模型进行微调以预测并发程序下一事件，在真实Go bug数据集上达到36.2%的准确率，优于Gemini 3.5 Flash零样本（34.8%）和未微调基线（28.6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测并发程序的下一步事件是一个难题：由于调度器的非确定性，同一程序的两次运行即使从相同的轨迹前缀开始，也可能产生不同的有效下一事件。传统方法训练模型匹配单一标签，实际上是在猜测随机过程的一个可能结果，这是不合理的。

Method: 将非确定性转化为训练信号：对每个程序运行多次，将观测到的下一事件聚合成经验分布，使用KL散度目标函数对7B模型进行微调，使其输出分布匹配经验分布。同时推导出一种形式化的goroutine泄漏签名，用于识别被select阻塞且调度器语义保证无法解除阻塞的goroutine。

Result: 在798个来自真实生产环境Go bug（CockroachDB、Kubernetes、gRPC、etcd）的测试样本上，使用少于1000条轨迹进行微调达到36.2%准确率，优于Gemini 3.5 Flash零样本（34.8%）和相同模型未微调版本（28.6%）。分布训练在准确率上与交叉熵相当（35.8% vs 36.2%），但将预期校准误差（ECE）从0.205降低至0.169。

Conclusion: 利用非确定性作为训练信号而非障碍，分布训练方法在并发程序预测任务上取得了竞争性性能，同时提升了概率校准能力。研究发布了数据集、训练适配器和全部工具。

Abstract: Training a model to predict the next step in a concurrent program is harder than it looks: two runs of the same program from the same trace prefix can produce different next events, both valid, because the scheduler is nondeterministic. A model trained against a single label is learning to guess one outcome of a random process. We turn this around and use the nondeterminism as a training signal. We run each program many times, aggregate the observed next events into an empirical distribution, and fine-tune a 7B model to match that distribution with a KL objective. On 798 held-out predictions drawn from real production Go bugs (CockroachDB, Kubernetes, gRPC, etcd), fine-tuning on fewer than a thousand traces reaches 36.2% accuracy, ahead of Gemini 3.5 Flash used zero-shot (34.8%) and the same model without fine-tuning (28.6%). Distribution training matches cross-entropy on accuracy (35.8% vs. 36.2%) while reducing Expected Calibration Error from 0.205 to 0.169. We also derive a formal goroutine-leak signature for a class of select-blocked goroutines where P(GoUnblock)=0 holds by scheduler semantics, not by learning. We release the dataset, trained adapters, and all tooling.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ELK问题中，行为评估无法检测明知真相却故意撒谎的模型。研究者探究这种欺骗行为是否会在模型内部留下区别于诚实错误的特征签名。

Method: 设计"错误性控制"实验：对比"潜伏代理"（知道真相、触发时撒谎）与"幼稚撒谎者"（经微调产生相同错误答案但无诚实训练），两者输出相同错误答案，差异仅在于知识冲突。分析残差秩差异，并在多种欺骗场景下验证签名的鲁棒性和跨模型迁移能力。

Result: 欺骗性前向传播的残差秩比幼稚撒谎者高2.1-2.3倍，无标签识别谎言准确率达100%（GPT-2 small/medium及三个指令模型）。在Qwen2.5和Phi-3-mini上，指令欺骗在所有测试事实上都提高残差秩。签名在自主构造欺骗（AUC 1.0）、主动隐瞒尝试（AUC 1.0）和长度控制复制中均保持完美分离。使用无基相对表示，在一个模型家族训练的探针可零样本检测其他家族的欺骗（平均AUC 0.933），跨五语言迁移（AUC 1.000）。签名只读不可注入。

Conclusion: 欺骗行为在模型内部留下可检测的冲突签名，该签名稳健、可跨架构和语言迁移，为检测AI模型故意欺骗提供了可行的内部状态分析方向。

Abstract: A model that lies while knowing the truth is the central case ELK cannot handle with behavioral evaluation alone. We ask whether such deception leaves an internal signature distinguishing it from honest error. Our key move is a control for wrongness: we contrast a sleeper agent (knows the truth, lies on trigger) against a naive liar (fine-tuned to emit the same wrong answers with no honest training). Both produce identical wrong outputs; any difference is about knowledge conflict, not incorrectness. We find deceptive forward passes carry a conflict signature - 2.1-2.3x higher residual rank than naive-liar passes on the same wrong answer - strong enough to identify which of two responses is the lie with 100% accuracy and no labels, across GPT-2 small/medium (three seeds) and three instruct models. Across Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B and Phi-3-mini, instructed deception raises residual rank on every tested fact (18/18, 40/40, 34/34); on Phi-3, lies separate perfectly from both honest answers and hallucinations (AUC 1.0, Wilcoxon p~6e-11). The signature survives strategic self-constructed deception (model invents its own lie, AUC 1.0), active concealment attempts (AUC 1.0), and length-controlled replication (20/20, AUC 1.0, p~1e-6). Using basis-free relative representations, a probe trained on one model family detects deception in two other families zero-shot (mean AUC 0.933), surviving simultaneous architecture and format change (AUC 0.821), and transfers across five languages (AUC 1.000, length-controlled). The signature is read-only: detectable but not injectable (0/8 both directions). Honest limitations and six negative experiments are documented in full.

</details>


### [35] [S4oP: Operator-level Pruning of Structured State Space Models for Resource-Constrained Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18096)
*Marco Deano,Filippo Ziche,Nicola Bombieri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出针对结构化状态空间模型(S4/S4D)的增量式算子剪枝方法，可在剪枝70%算子的情况下保持模型性能并显著降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化状态空间模型(SSMs)虽然在捕获长程依赖关系方面表现优异，但其较高的计算和内存需求限制了在时间和资源受限场景下的部署，亟需一种有效的模型压缩方法来提升部署效率。

Method: 提出一种增量式算子级剪枝方法，通过将结构化掩码与微调交替进行，同时监控准确率和推理延迟来逐步剪枝模型算子；在统一的训练和评估框架中实现该方法，系统地探索效率与准确率之间的权衡。
