TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于同伦类型论的神经符号(NeSy)系统框架，通过用类型替代集合来保留对称性信息，解决了传统方法忽略的对称性问题，从而更好地处理推理捷径并提高模型校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经符号系统基于集合计算，但集合会遗忘两类重要信息：理论对称性下的等价结构以及查询的独立证明数量。这些被遗忘的信息对神经符号推理至关重要，特别是对称性信息与推理捷径问题直接相关。

Method: 作者提出用同伦类型论中的"类型"替代传统框架中的"集合"，将原有的信念加权求和函数转化为信念加权同伦基数。这种基数概念按照对象对称性的倒数来计数每个对象，从而自然保留对称性信息。作者从零开始构建了完整的框架，并证明了保守性定理。

Result: 在MNIST推理捷径基准测试上，该单模型包装器在保持标签准确率和可识别概念不变的情况下，校准性能优于经过多样性训练的集成方法。作者证明了框架暴露的对称性正是推理捷径背后的对称性，并给出了捷径感知概念后验的闭式解。

Conclusion: 通过引入同伦类型论处理对称性问题，本文框架成功解决了神经符号系统中的推理捷径问题。捷径感知概念后验是对称群的唯一对称不变点，可通过单个模型在对称群上平均得到闭式解，为神经符号系统提供了理论上更优雅且实践中更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: A wide range of neurosymbolic (NeSy) systems compute one functional: a belief-weighted sum of a logical quantity over a space of $σ$-structures, of which weighted model counting, fuzzy logic, and probabilistic logic are special cases. This account is built on sets, and a set deliberately forgets two things that are important for NeSy: when two $σ$-structures are the same up to a symmetry of the theory, and how many distinct proofs witness a query. Replacing the underlying sets by types, in the sense of homotopy type theory, preserves this information, and turns this functional into a belief-weighted homotopy cardinality, a notion of size that counts each object in inverse proportion to its symmetries. We develop the framework from scratch for NeSy systems, prove a conservativity theorem that recovers the classical functional when symmetries are trivial, and show that the symmetry our framework exposes is exactly the one behind reasoning shortcuts. The payoff is concrete: the shortcut-aware concept posterior that recent methods reach by ensembling or expressive density estimation is the only symmetry-invariant point of the confusion-set simplex, computable in closed form by averaging a single model over the symmetry group. On MNIST reasoning-shortcut benchmarks this single-model wrapper is better calibrated than a diversity-trained ensemble, while leaving label accuracy and identifiable concepts untouched. Code is freely available at https://github.com/bio-ontology-research-group/hott-nesy.

</details>


### [147] [FlowRAG: Synergizing Explicit Reasoning via Frequency-Aware Multi-Granularity Graph Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17856)
*Bihao Zhan,Zongsheng Cao,Jie Zhou,Bo Zhang,Liang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlowRAG通过构建四层异构图（段落、摘要、句子、实体）和双重粒度激活模块，解决了现有GraphRAG方法在抽象查询下的检索不足和多跳推理脆弱性问题，实现了更准确的语义召回和显式推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GraphRAG方法主要基于实体构建图并依赖隐式语义相关性传播，存在两个关键问题：（1）当用户查询抽象且在实体层面语义稀疏时检索不足；（2）多跳推理脆弱，噪声激活会干扰实体间转换并破坏推理链，导致不可靠的结论。

Method: 提出FlowRAG框架：1）构建包含段落、摘要、句子、实体的四层异构图，摘要节点作为粗粒度语义枢纽；2）双重粒度激活模块结合摘要-查询对齐和句子级匹配，在释义和抽象场景下鲁棒地激活相关实体；3）频率感知加权流模块，通过段落内词频对实体-段落链接进行加权路由相关性，剪枝噪声连接并提取高置信度推理路径作为生成的显式逻辑骨架。

Result: 在复杂推理基准测试上进行了广泛实验，FlowRAG达到了最先进的性能表现。

Conclusion: FlowRAG通过语义感知的四层异构图构建、双重粒度激活和频率感知加权流机制，有效提升了GraphRAG在语义召回和多跳推理方面的能力，为知识密集型复杂查询任务提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Graph-based retrieval-augmented generation (GraphRAG) is effective for knowledge-intensive and multi-hop query tasks; however, many existing methods primarily seed entity-based graphs and rely on implicit semantic relevance propagation. This often (i) under-retrieves when user queries are abstract and semantically sparse at the entity level, and (ii) suffers from brittle multi-hop reasoning, where noisy activations can derail entity-to-entity transitions and corrupt the inferred relation chain, yielding unreliable conclusions. To this end, we propose \texttt{FlowRAG}, a semantic-aware retrieval framework that improves both semantic recall and explicit reasoning. Specifically, \texttt{FlowRAG} constructs a quad-level heterogeneous graph over passages, summaries, sentences, and entities, where summary nodes serve as a coarse semantic hub. At retrieval time, a dual-granularity activation module combines summary--query alignment with sentence-level matching to activate relevant entities under paraphrase and abstraction robustly. We then introduce a frequency-aware weighted flow module that routes relevance through entity--passage links weighted by within-passage term frequency, pruning noisy connections and extracting high-confidence reasoning paths as an explicit logic skeleton for generation. Extensive experiments show that \texttt{FlowRAG} obtains state-of-the-art performance on complex reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [148] [StepGuard: Guarding Web Navigation via Single-Step Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17871)
*Zhihao Cui,Yuchen Zhang,Xiyang Sun,Yaxiong Wang,Li Zhu,Jinpeng Hu,Liu Liu,Mengjia Li,Yujiao Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了StepGuard框架，通过动态双策略优化（DDPO）和置信度引导的自适应导航反思（CANR）机制，解决了网页导航任务中的奖励错位和单步误差累积问题，实现了新的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的网页导航方法存在两个关键问题：（1）奖励错位导致的单步脆弱性；（2）误差传播问题。这些问题源于奖励纠缠和缺乏有效的单步纠错机制，严重影响了导航和问答的准确性。

Method: 提出了两个核心组件：（1）动态双策略优化（DDPO）：在导航优先模式（用于探索）和答案优先模式（用于问答）之间动态切换，缓解奖励冲突；（2）置信度引导的自适应导航反思（CANR）：估计每步置信度，仅在必要时触发反思，使用对比奖励鼓励自我纠正，校准单步不准确性。基于这些组件构建了StepGuard框架。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在导航准确性和答案准确性方面均有显著提升，在标准网页导航基准测试上达到了新的最先进（SOTA）性能。

Conclusion: 通过解决奖励错位和单步误差校准问题，StepGuard框架有效提升了网页导航任务的性能，为基于视觉语言模型和强化学习的网页导航代理提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Web navigation requires agents to follow natural language goals, interact with web pages, and produce accurate answers. While recent advances leverage vision-language models and reinforcement learning, existing methods still suffer from single-step fragility due to reward misalignment and error propagation. To tackle the reward entanglement, we design Dynamic Dual-Policy Optimization (DDPO), which dynamically switches between a navigation-first mode for exploration and an answer-first mode for question-answering to mitigate reward conflict. To calibrate the single-step error, we propose Confidence-Guided Adaptive Navigation Reflection (CANR), a mechanism that estimates per-step confidence, triggers reflection only when necessary, and uses contrastive rewards to encourage self-correction to calibrate the single-step inaccuracy. With the above as the main components, we finally develop our StepGuard, a new framework of Guarding Web Navigation via Single-Step Calibration. Experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly improves navigation and answer accuracy, setting new state-of-the-art performance on standard web navigation benchmarks.

</details>


### [149] [Structural Preservation and the Logical Expressiveness of Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17882)
*Przemysław Andrzej Wałęga,Bernardo Cuenca Grau*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从语义角度建立了GNN分类器与分级模态逻辑片段之间的对应关系，证明了在嵌入、单射同态和同态下保持的结构性质分别对应存在分级模态逻辑及其不同片段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通过固定架构选择（如聚合、组合和激活函数类型）建立GNN与逻辑形式之间的桥梁，但这些对应关系依赖于特定的架构约束。本文旨在从语义视角出发，独立于具体架构选择，刻画在结构性质下保持的GNN类的表达能力。

Method: 采用语义视角，研究在嵌入（扩张）、单射同态和同态等结构性质下保持的GNN分类器类。利用关于有界高度树的新型良拟序结果，获得解卷不变类的有限表示，从而建立GNN类与分级模态逻辑片段之间的对应关系。

Result: 证明了每个结构保持性质对应一个分级模态逻辑片段：嵌入保持对应存在分级模态逻辑；单射同态保持对应其存在正片段；同态保持对应存在正模态逻辑。同时证明每个逻辑片段都存在具有相同表达能力的GNN架构。

Conclusion: 成功刻画了广泛GNN类的表达能力，无需依赖特定架构选择，同时为每类逻辑片段提供了等价表达的GNN架构，为理解GNN的逻辑表达能力提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Bridges between graph neural networks (GNNs) and logical formalisms have been established by fixing architectural choices, such as the types of aggregation, combination, and activation functions. These choices define restricted classes of GNNs for which tight correspondences with logical formalisms can be obtained, by showing that logical formulae can be translated into equivalent GNNs and, conversely, that GNNs can be translated into equivalent formulae.
  In this paper we take a semantic perspective by establishing the logical expressiveness of classes of GNN classifiers that are preserved under structural properties: embeddings (extensions), injective homomorphisms, and homomorphisms. We show that, for each such property, there exists a fragment of graded modal logic characterising the class of GNNs. In particular, preservation under embeddings, injective homomorphisms, and homomorphisms corresponds to existential graded modal logic, its existential-positive fragment, and existential-positive modal logic, respectively. These results characterise the expressiveness of broad classes of GNNs independently of specific architectural choices, but we also show that each of these classes admits a GNN architecture of the same expressiveness.
  Technically, our approach uses a new well-quasi-order result for trees of bounded height, yielding finite representations of unravelling-invariant classes.

</details>


### [150] [MathVis-Fine: Aligning Visual Supervision with Necessity via Progressive Dependency-Guided Training for Multimodal Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17888)
*Wanshi Xu,Haokun Zhao,Haidong Yuan,Songjun Cao,Long Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MathVis-Fine框架，通过构建细粒度视觉依赖标注数据集和两阶段渐进式视觉增强训练范式，解决了多模态数学推理中视觉监督信号粗糙和训练反馈不准确的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Chain-of-Thought推理方法在多模态场景下将视觉输入视为同质化或辅助信号，无法捕捉数学问题求解中文本与图像之间复杂且样本特定的依赖关系。这导致两个核心问题：(1)视觉内容的监督信号泛化且粗糙，缺乏对样本中视觉信息实际必要性的适应；(2)当视觉奖励被均匀应用时，训练反馈变得不准确。

Method: (1)构建MathVis-Fine数据集，为数学问题添加细粒度视觉标注和视觉依赖评分；(2)提出两阶段渐进式视觉增强训练范式，根据每个样本内在的视觉依赖水平平衡答案正确性奖励和视觉基础奖励，从而缓解奖励偏差并提高监督准确性。

Result: 大量实验表明，MathVis-Fine框架能够基于视觉依赖有效地渐进增强视觉感知，为多模态数学推理提供了更精确的训练框架。数据集将在论文接收后发布。

Conclusion: 该工作通过建模细粒度视觉依赖，成功提升了多模态数学推理模型的视觉感知能力和推理精度，为未来多模态推理研究提供了新的思路和数据资源。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning has extended from purely linguistic domains to multimodal scenarios; however, existing approaches often treat visual inputs as homogeneous or auxiliary signals, failing to capture the intricate and sample-specific dependencies between text and images in mathematical problem-solving. This gives rise to two core issues: first, the supervisory signals for visual content are generalized and coarse-grained, lacking adaptation to the actual necessity of visual information in each sample; second, training feedback becomes inaccurate when visual rewards are uniformly applied without distinguishing the complementary relationships among inputs. These limitations hinder models from achieving precise multimodal reasoning. In this work, we propose a framework for modeling fine-grained visual dependencies in mathematical reasoning. We first construct the MathVis-Fine dataset, augmenting fine-grained visual annotations with visual dependency ratings. Building upon this dataset, we introduce a two-stage progressive visual enhancement training paradigm that balances answer correctness rewards and visual grounding rewards according to the intrinsic visual dependency level of each sample, thereby mitigating reward bias and improving supervision accuracy. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the MathVis-Fine framework effectively enhances visual perception progressively based on visual dependency, offering a more precise training framework for multimodal mathematical reasoning. We will release the dataset upon acceptance.

</details>


### [151] [Learn to Quantify Social Interaction with Constraints for Pedestrian Walking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17897)
*Xiaodan Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Learn to Cluster方法，通过概率潜在变量生成模型量化并解释人群中的社会交互，显著提升了行人长期路径预测的鲁棒性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽考虑社会交互，但未揭示交互的具体类型及其对行人决策过程的影响，且社会交互难以标注和量化，这限制了预测模型的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出Learn to Cluster方法，采用概率潜在变量生成模型，直接从轨迹序列观测中无监督地学习社会交互模式；该方法是标签无关的，可自然集成到预测模型的训练过程中，潜在变量作为交互类别的“标签”。

Result: 在多个轨迹预测基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法能有效学习社会交互模式，并将这些模式有效整合到行人轨迹预测中。

Conclusion: Learn to Cluster为量化社会交互提供了一种可扩展的无监督解决方案，有望提升自主移动平台的规划质量与安全性。

Abstract: Long-term human path forecasting in crowds is critical for autonomous moving platforms (like autonomous driving cars and social robots) to avoid collision and make high-quality planning. Although the current research take into account social interactions for prediction, they don't reveal the exact kinds of social interactions happened among people and how the social interactions affect the decision-making process of pedestrians, which further limits its robustness. Social interactions in pedestrian walking are intuitively massive and hard to label and quantify. In this paper, we explore creatively to quantify and interpret how pedestrians interact with others by proposing Learn to Cluster. Our clustering social interactions is probabilistic latent variable generative, learning directly from sequential trajectory observations, scalable to arbitrary number of pedestrians. Learn to cluster is label-free and can be naturally integrated into the training process of the prediction model. The latent variables will then serve as 'labels' to categorize social interactions. Extensive experiments over several trajectory prediction benchmarks demonstrate that our method is able to learn the patterns of social interactions and effectively integrate the patterns to pedestrian trajectory prediction.

</details>


### [152] [DiagFlowBench: Evaluating How Language Models Handle Off-Procedure Inputs in Grounded Diagnostic Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17904)
*Guillermo Gil de Avalle,Laura Maruster,Shaina Raza,Christos Emmanouilidis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DiagFlowBench数据集，用于评估语言模型在工业诊断对话中识别超出范围输入的能力，发现模型倾向于选择看似合理但上下文不当的建议而非编造事实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型作为维护操作的顾问系统，虽然通过程序文档约束来防止幻觉，但在实际操作中，操作员的查询经常偏离既定流程。现有基准测试很少优先考虑模型在对话过程中识别超出范围输入的能力，这一动态需求促使了本研究。

Method: 构建DiagFlowBench数据集，将来自消费制造商的50个工业诊断流程图转换为1,676个多轮对话，这些对话对比了符合规范的回答和超出范围的输入。使用该数据集评估了10个商业和开源权重模型的表现。

Result: 评估结果显示模型在拒绝回答率方面存在高度变异性。模型通常选择一个真实但上下文不当的步骤，而不是编造事实。这种映射但错误的建议具有内在合理性和权威性，暴露了基于文档约束系统的关键漏洞。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了一个重要的安全问题：模型倾向于给出看似合理但实际不当的建议，这种行为比直接编造事实更难被检测，对基于文档约束的系统构成了挑战性漏洞。

Abstract: Language models increasingly serve as advisory systems in maintenance operations. To prevent hallucination, recent systems ground these models in procedural documentation to constrain them to approved steps. In practice, however, operator queries frequently stray from this path, requiring models to recognise out-of-scope inputs mid-conversation, a dynamic that current benchmarks rarely prioritise. We introduce DiagFlowBench, a dataset of 50 industrial diagnostic flowcharts from a consumer manufacturer converted into 1,676 multi-turn conversations that contrast compliant with out-of-scope utterances. Evaluating a panel of ten commercial and open-weight models reveals high variability in abstention rates, with models commonly selecting a real but contextually inadequate step rather than fabricating facts. The inherent plausibility and authority of this mapped but wrong advice exposes a challenging vulnerability for grounding systems.

</details>


### [153] [PreAct: Computer-Using Agents that Get Faster on Repeated Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17929)
*Bojie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PreAct将智能体成功执行的任务编译为状态机程序，在重复执行时直接回放，实现8.5-13倍加速，通过屏幕状态检查确保回放可靠性，并通过独立验证机制过滤无效程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用智能体每次执行任务都从头开始，即使重复任务也需要重新读取屏幕、重新推理每一步操作，导致重复的计算开销和延迟。

Method: 首次成功执行任务时，将运行过程编译为小型状态机程序（状态检查屏幕，转换执行动作）；后续运行直接回放程序而不调用智能体。回放时每步检查屏幕状态是否匹配预期，不匹配则交回控制权。仅当独立评估器确认程序能从干净状态成功完成任务时，才将其存入程序库。

Result: 在移动端、桌面端和网页端三个基准测试上，重复任务执行速度提升8.5-13倍；存储时检查机制避免了故障程序累积，每个基准测试净提升1.75-2.6个任务；当无匹配程序时回退到重新探索，使PreAct与强大的记录-回放基线持平。

Conclusion: PreAct通过编译-回放机制有效解决了智能体重复任务执行效率问题，同时通过状态检查和独立验证确保了回放的可靠性和正确性。提示词措辞、运行时防护栏以及程序检索方式（语言模型还是嵌入检索器）对性能影响不显著。

Abstract: Computer-using agents drive real software through the screen -- clicking and typing -- but they solve every task from scratch: asked to repeat a task, an agent re-reads the screen, re-reasons every tap, and pays the full cost again. We present PreAct, which lets such an agent get faster on tasks it has done before. The first time it succeeds, PreAct compiles the run into a small state-machine program-states that check the screen, transitions that act-and on later runs replays it directly instead of invoking the agent 8.5-13x faster, with no per-step language-model calls. Replay is not blind: at each step PreAct checks that the screen matches what the program expects before acting, and hands control back to the agent the moment something is off.
  PreAct applies the same discipline when deciding what to keep: a freshly compiled program enters the store only if, re-run from a clean state, an independent evaluator confirms it solved the task-catching programs that replay to their last step yet leave the task undone. Across a mobile, a desktop, and a web benchmark, this store-time check separates repeated runs that improve from ones that degrade as faulty programs accumulate, worth 1.75-2.6 tasks per benchmark, the same direction on all three; a fallback that explores afresh when no program fits brings PreAct level with a strong record-and-replay baseline. We also report what did not matter: prompt wording, runtime guardrails, and whether a language model or a plain embedding retriever selects which program to reuse.

</details>


### [154] [How Inference Compute Shapes Frontier LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17930)
*Jessica McFadyen,Ole Jorgensen,Harry Coppock,Kevin Wei,Cozmin Ududec*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现基准测试分数依赖于推理计算预算，更大的token预算能显著提升模型在多个领域的表现，固定预算评估会低估先进模型的真实能力，因此建议报告能力随推理计算变化的函数关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI评估任务日益复杂，需要更长的推理轨迹和工具使用，模型性能对测试时可用的计算量（推理计算）越来越敏感。然而许多评估仍在单一限制性预算下报告性能，导致低分可能反映的是评估设置而非模型真实能力，需要研究推理计算对评估结果的影响。

Method: 在七个挑战性基准测试（涵盖软件工程、数学、医学和网络安全）上评估多达12个前沿语言模型。采用控制性实验设计，结合三种简单推理扩展干预措施：更大的token预算、上下文压缩和重复提交尝试，由模型自身或最小正确性反馈引导。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）更大的token预算在多个领域的基准测试中显著提升性能，包括网络安全、FrontierMath、Humanity's Last Exam和TerminalBench；（2）固定预算评估会随着模型进步越来越低估前沿能力，新模型在大预算下达到更高性能，能解锁更难任务并更可靠地解决它们；（3）不同基准测试适用的推理扩展方法不同：重复提交普遍改善性能，但大token预算、外部反馈和并行尝试的价值因基准而异。

Conclusion: 基准测试分数具有协议依赖性，评估应报告能力作为推理时间计算的函数，明确指定协议选择，并在匹配预算的大共享计算范围内比较模型世代，特别是在安全或政策相关场景中。

Abstract: AI evaluations are shifting toward harder tasks that benefit from longer trajectories involving tool use and iterative problem solving. As a result, performance is increasingly sensitive to the amount and allocation of compute available at test time ("inference compute"). Yet many evaluations still report performance at a single restrictive budget, meaning that low scores may reflect the evaluation setup rather than the model's underlying capability. To test this, we evaluate up to 12 frontier language models on seven challenging benchmarks spanning software engineering, mathematics, medicine, and cybersecurity. We use a controlled setup combining three simple inference-scaling interventions: larger token budgets, context compaction, and repeated submission attempts, guided either by the model itself or by minimal correctness feedback. We find three main results. First, larger token budgets substantially improve performance on benchmarks across multiple domains, including cybersecurity, FrontierMath, Humanity's Last Exam, and TerminalBench. Second, fixed-budget evaluations can increasingly understate frontier capability as models advance. Newer models reach higher performance at large budgets, where they unlock harder tasks and solve them more reliably. Third, benchmarks differ in which inference-scaling methods help most: repeated submission broadly improves performance, but the value of larger token budgets, external feedback, and parallel attempts varies by benchmark. Overall, our results show that benchmark scores are protocol-dependent. We therefore argue that evaluations should report capability as a function of inference-time compute, specify protocol choices explicitly, and compare model generations over a large shared compute range at matched budgets, especially in safety- or policy-relevant settings.

</details>


### [155] [Small Initialization Matters for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17945)
*Liangkai Hang,Junjie Yao,Zhiyu Li,Feiyu Xiong,Hongkang Yang,Zhi-Qin John Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 发现参数初始化是影响LLM训练和模型能力的关键因素。减小初始化规模能持续改善预训练效果，特别是在需要推理的任务上，并提出了一种几乎零成本的γ-初始化规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索智能本身如何涌现，而不仅仅是工程化LLM。尽管模型进步通常归功于规模、数据和架构，作者希望揭示参数初始化作为类似基因的决定性因素，对训练过程和模型能力的根本影响。

Method: 1) 系统研究初始化规模对预训练的影响；2) 识别并调整限制小初始化优势的经验设置；3) 发现平衡推理与训练的关键初始化点；4) 通过参数轨迹和token级分析，从机制层面解释小初始化的作用原理；5) 提出γ-初始化规则。

Result: 1) 减小初始化规模持续改善预训练，在推理密集型任务上提升最大；2) 小初始化驱动独特的发育轨迹：参数先凝聚成低复杂度结构，再扩展为更丰富的表征；3) 性能提升集中在非平凡、上下文约束的预测上，而非均匀分布在所有token上。

Conclusion: 初始化规模是影响模型训练和能力涌现的关键因素。提出简单实用的γ-初始化规则：将初始化范围作为显式调节旋钮，默认使用小初始化。这是一种几乎零成本的干预措施，能改善预训练效果并增强各规模模型的推理能力，验证了"压缩即智能"的理念。

Abstract: Large language models provide a tractable system for asking how intelligence itself emerges, rather than only how LLMs can be engineered. Although progress is usually attributed to scale, data and architecture, we show that parameter initialization is a gene-like determinant of training and, in particular, of model capacity. Reducing the initialization scale consistently improves pretraining, with the largest gains on reasoning-demanding tasks. We identify two widely used empirical settings that restrain the advantage of small initialization, and show how relaxing them restores favorable scaling. We further uncover a critical initialization that balances the reasoning and training. Mechanistically, small initialization drives a distinct developmental trajectory: parameters first condense into low-complexity structures and later expand into richer representations, giving concrete form to the idea that compression is intelligence. Token-level analyses show that the gains concentrate on non-trivial, context-constrained predictions rather than all tokens uniformly. These results motivate a simple $γ$-initialization rule: expose initialization rage as an explicit knob and use small initialization by default, an almost cost-free intervention that improves pretraining and strengthens reasoning across model scales.

</details>


### [156] [MoCo-AIS: A Contrastive Learning Framework for Similarity Computation of Vessel Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17978)
*Ruixin Song,Md Mahbub Alam,Zahra Sadeghi,Amilcar Soares,José F. Rodrigues-Jr,Gabriel Spadon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MoCo-AIS框架，这是一个基于动量对比学习范式的统一框架，用于学习船舶轨迹嵌入表示，解决了现有方法计算成本高、泛化能力差和缺乏统一评估平台的问题，在大规模AIS数据集上显著提升了轨迹相似性学习的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于距离的轨迹相似性度量方法计算成本高昂；现有的监督学习方法依赖大量标签且泛化能力有限；自监督学习方法虽然解决了部分问题，但缺乏统一框架，难以对不同深度学习模型进行一致的轨迹表示比较和评估。

Method: 提出了MoCo-AIS统一框架，基于动量对比学习范式，通过正负轨迹对来构建相似性学习任务。该框架能够在统一的标准下评估各种主流深度学习模型，用于学习船舶轨迹的嵌入表示。

Result: 在大规模真实世界的船舶自动识别系统（AIS）数据集上进行了广泛实验，这些数据集涵盖了多样化的航行行为和操作条件。结果表明，MoCo-AIS框架在轨迹相似性学习方面显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: MoCo-AIS框架不仅有效提升了轨迹相似性学习的性能，还为轨迹表示模型的评估提供了一个标准化的基准测试平台，有助于推动该领域的发展。

Abstract: Trajectory similarity is a fundamental task in analyzing mobility patterns, essential for applications such as route pattern extraction, mobility prediction, and anomaly detection. Traditional distance-based measures for computing similarity incur high computational cost, driving the adoption of lightweight learning-based approaches. Supervised methods rely on extensive labels derived from traditional distance measures and often reproduce these metrics, which limits generalization. While self-supervised learning addresses this issue through contrastive learning, it lacks a unified framework, making it difficult to compare deep learning (DL) models for consistent trajectory representation. Accordingly, this paper presents MoCo-AIS, a unified framework for learning vessel trajectory embeddings based on the Momentum Contrast (MoCo) paradigm, which formulates similarity learning through positive and negative trajectory pairs. Within this framework, we evaluate a diverse set of leading DL models on large-scale, real-world vessel-tracking AIS datasets that capture diverse navigation behaviors and operating conditions. Results demonstrate that our framework significantly improves similarity learning over existing baselines, while providing a benchmarking platform for evaluating trajectory representation models.

</details>


### [157] [STAR: SpatioTemporal Adaptive Reward Allocation for Text-to-Image RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17979)
*Jinjie Shen,Wei Deng,Xian Hu,Daiguo Zhou,Jian Luan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出STAR（时空自适应奖励分配）方法，通过在去噪过程的不同步骤和图像不同区域动态分配奖励权重，解决了RL后训练中奖励粒度不匹配的问题，显著提升了文本到图像生成的文本对齐、文本渲染和偏好优化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RL后训练方法将最终图像奖励转换为单一标量优势值，并以相同强度应用于整个生成轨迹。然而，文本到图像生成具有天然的时空结构：不同去噪步骤负责不同生成阶段，且决定文本对齐的核心内容通常仅出现在图像部分区域。这种粒度不匹配导致策略更新难以聚焦于真正影响奖励的生成组件。

Method: 提出STAR（时空自适应奖励分配）方法：利用生成模型内部的文本-图像注意力机制，从用户真正关注的提示核心内容出发，构建随去噪步骤和推演动态变化的空间分配图；将相同的组相对优势值以几乎无额外计算开销的方式分配给更相关的潜空间区域；通过空间分辨的策略目标对这些区域施加更强的策略更新。使用Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium作为基础模型。

Result: 在GenEval、OCR文本渲染和PickScore三个任务上评估，在不改变外部奖励源的情况下，STAR分别达到0.9759、0.9757和23.60的分数，在组合语义对齐、文本渲染和偏好优化方面均有提升。

Conclusion: STAR通过时空自适应的奖励分配机制，成功解决了RL后训练中的粒度不匹配问题，使策略更新能够聚焦于真正影响奖励的生成组件，在多个评估维度上取得了性能提升，为文本到图像扩散模型和流模型的RL后训练提供了有效的新范式。

Abstract: Existing RL post-training methods for text-to-image generation usually convert the final-image reward into a single scalar advantage and apply it with the same strength to the entire generative trajectory. However, text-to-image generation naturally has temporal and spatial structure: different denoising steps are responsible for different generation stages, and the content that truly determines text alignment often appears only in part of the image. This granularity mismatch makes it difficult for policy updates to focus on the generative components that actually affect the reward. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{SpatioTemporal Adaptive Reward (STAR) Allocation} for RL post-training of text-to-image diffusion and flow models. STAR uses text-image attention inside the generative model and starts from the core content that the user truly cares about in the prompt. It constructs spatial allocation maps that dynamically vary across denoising steps and rollouts, and allocates the same group-relative advantage to more relevant latent regions with almost no additional computational overhead. STAR then applies stronger policy updates to these regions through a spatially resolved policy objective. We use Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium as the base model and evaluate on three tasks: GenEval, OCR text rendering, and PickScore. Experimental results show that STAR improves compositional semantic alignment, text rendering, and preference optimization without changing the external reward source, achieving $\mathbf{0.9759}$, $\mathbf{0.9757}$, and $\mathbf{23.60}$ on GenEval, OCR, and PickScore, respectively.

</details>


### [158] [LLM Consumer Behavior Theory: Foundations of a Novel Research Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18005)
*Manon Reusens,Sofie Goethals,David Martens*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“LLM消费者行为理论”新框架，探讨大语言模型作为自主代理代替人类做消费决策时，对传统消费者理论的影响，并指出理性与异质性等假设可能在代理市场中失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）越来越多地被部署为自主代理，代表用户进行消费决策，这一转变对传统以人类为主要决策者的消费者理论提出了根本性问题，促使需要新的理论框架来分析与理解代理市场中的消费者行为。

Method: 借鉴经典经济学与行为经济学理论，结合自然语言处理领域的最新进展，本文形式化了人类偏好如何被LLM代理反映与执行，以及代理层面的决策如何聚合为市场需求；并将此前分散的LLM决策、行为模拟与偏好 elicitation 研究统一在经济视角下进行整合。

Result: 本文确立了LLM消费者行为理论的研究范畴，指出了理性与异质性等经典假设在代理市场中可能失效的关键点，并围绕对齐、偏好表示与市场动态识别了重要的开放研究问题。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解代理市场中的消费行为奠定了理论基础，强调未来需在模型与市场设计中对偏好对齐与行为异质性给予更多关注，以应对代理决策与传统消费者理论间的本质差异。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents that make consumption decisions on behalf of users. This shift raises fundamental questions for consumer theory, which has traditionally modeled humans as the primary decision-makers. In this paper, we introduce LLM Consumer Behavior Theory, a new field of study concerned with analyzing consumer behavior in agentic markets. Drawing on classical and behavioral economics alongside recent advances in Natural Language Processing, we formalize how human preferences are reflected and acted upon by LLM-based agents, and how agent-level decisions aggregate into market demand. We unify previously fragmented literature on LLM decision-making, human behavior simulation, and preference elicitation under a common economic lens, highlighting where assumptions, such as rationality and heterogeneity, may fail in agentic markets. Rather than providing empirical validation, this paper outlines the scope of LLM consumer behavior and identifies open research questions related to alignment, preference representation, and market dynamics.

</details>


### [159] [PseudoBench: Measuring How Agentic Auto-Research Fuels Pseudoscience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18060)
*Xinyang Liao,Lingyu Li,Huacan Liu,Tianle Gu,Yang Yao,Tong Zhu,Yan Teng,Yingchun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PseudoBench基准，用于评估自主科研AI系统识别和抵制伪科学的能力。测试7个先进代理发现，当前系统几乎不拒绝伪科学前提，最高抵制率仅27.4%，且更强的代理会用更专业的科学语言包装伪科学，增加其可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于大语言模型的自主科研代理进入科学研究领域，其抵制伪科学的能力至关重要。若缺乏此能力，这些系统可能快速生成看似合理但具有误导性的研究，污染学术文献并侵蚀对科学的信任。

Method: 提出PseudoBench——一个对抗性基准，包含跨五个领域的200个精心策划的伪科学声明-证据对。该基准通过从实验到写作的端到端研究流程来评估代理系统。对七个最先进的代理进行了测试。

Result: 当前系统很容易生成与伪科学前提一致的、具有说服力的报告，拒绝率接近零，最高抵制率仅为27.4%。更强的代理存在用更复杂的科学语言包装伪科学的风险，从而增加其表面可信度。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了自主科研代理助长伪科学的令人担忧的能力，在大规模部署之前迫切需要进行科学对齐，以确保系统不会加剧伪科学传播。

Abstract: As Large Language Model based agents enter autonomous scientific research, their ability to resist pseudoscience becomes increasingly important. Otherwise, such systems may rapidly generate plausible yet misleading studies that contaminate academic literature and erode trust in science. We present PseudoBench, an adversarial benchmark for evaluating whether agentic auto-research systems can identify and resist pseudoscientific narratives. PseudoBench contains 200 curated pseudoscientific claim-evidence pairs across five domains and evaluates agents through an end-to-end research pipeline from experiments to writing. Testing seven state-of-the-art agents, we find that current systems readily produce persuasive reports that align with pseudoscientific premises with near-zero refusal rates and the highest resistance of only 27.4%. Stronger agents risk packaging pseudoscience in more sophisticated scientific language, increasing its apparent credibility. These findings reveal an alarming capacity to fuel pseudoscience, calling for scientific alignment before widespread deployment.

</details>


### [160] [Agentic AI-based Framework for Mitigating Premature Diagnostic Handoff and Silent Hallucination in Healthcare Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18068)
*Divyansh Srivastava,Shreya Ghosh,Anshul Verma,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于医疗诊断的多智能体框架，通过引入神经符号状态跟踪和认知不确定性量化两种安全机制，解决了过早诊断移交和临床幻觉问题，将诊断准确率提升了11.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型和多智能体系统在医疗推理方面展现出潜力，但开放式对话智能体仍存在两个关键失效模式：过早的诊断移交以及在到达患者前可能未被检测到的隐性临床幻觉，这些问题严重影响了医疗AI的安全性和可靠性。

Method: 提出一个多智能体框架，采用确定性编排约束替代"LLM作为评判者"的路由方式。框架包含两个安全机制：(1) 神经符号状态跟踪门，强制执行OLDCARTS临床协议的完整性，在收集所有必需维度信息前阻止诊断转换；(2) 认知不确定性量化门，通过计算K=5个独立诊断样本的语义熵(H)来识别和拦截发散性输出。使用llama-3.1-70b-instruct模型在150个测试案例上进行评估。

Result: 完整架构实现了49.3%的诊断准确率，相比无约束基线绝对提升了11.3个百分点。研究发现OLDCARTS完整性与语义熵之间存在统计学显著的负相关关系(r = -0.181, p < 0.05)，表明结构化信息收集与诊断不确定性的降低相关联。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了通过确定性编排约束和结构化临床协议可以有效提升医疗诊断智能体的安全性和准确性。神经符号状态跟踪与不确定性量化的结合为构建可靠的医疗AI系统提供了有前景的技术路径，结构化信息收集对降低诊断不确定性具有重要作用。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) and multi-agent systems have driven the rise of Agentic AI, showing promise for medical reasoning. However, open-ended conversational agents remain prone to two critical failure modes: premature diagnostic handoff and silent clinical hallucinations that may go undetected before reaching the patient. In this work, we propose a multi-agent framework that addresses both issues by replacing ``LLM-as-a-judge'' routing with deterministic orchestration constraints. The framework incorporates two safety mechanisms. First, a neuro-symbolic state-tracking gate enforces completeness of the OLDCARTS clinical protocol (Onset, Location, Duration, Character, Aggravating/Alleviating factors, Radiation, Timing, and Severity) by blocking diagnostic transitions until all required dimensions are collected. Second, an epistemic uncertainty quantification (UQ) gate computes semantic entropy (H) across K=5 independent diagnostic samples to identify and intercept divergent outputs before delivery.
  We evaluate the system using simulated patient agents powered by the llama-3.1-70b-instruct model on 150 test cases. The full architecture achieves 49.3% diagnostic precision, representing an absolute improvement of 11.3 percentage points over an unconstrained baseline. Additionally, we observe a statistically significant negative correlation (r = -0.181, p < 0.05) between OLDCARTS completeness (σ) and semantic entropy (H), suggesting that structured information gathering is associated with reduced diagnostic uncertainty.

</details>


### [161] [A Unified Framework for Context-Aware and Relation-Aware Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18075)
*Haoyang Zhong,Yifei Sun,Antong Zhang,Chunping Wang,Lei Chen,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HyGRAG框架，通过构建层次化混合图索引和迭代聚类生成LLM摘要，实现上下文与关系感知的检索，在多跳推理任务上平均准确率提升9.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图RAG方法存在根本局限：以实体为中心和以片段为中心的方法均锚定于原始文本表示，缺乏真正的知识融合。虽然前者连接逻辑相关内容，后者保留上下文，但两者通过相似性搜索独立检索信息，错失了综合后涌现的理解能力。

Method: 设计混合图上的层次化索引结构（包含片段节点和实体节点），迭代聚类并生成基于LLM的摘要；设计上下文与关系感知检索机制，在所有抽象层级搜索并通过社区成员关系扩展；提出基于附加的算法实现动态知识更新，仅需局部重摘要。

Result: HyGRAG在多跳推理任务上平均准确率提升9.7%，同时保持合理的效率。

Conclusion: HyGRAG通过层次化图结构和知识融合机制，有效解决了现有图RAG方法的局限性，实现了对动态语料库的高效处理和检索性能的显著提升。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has emerged as a paradigm for enhancing large language models (LLMs) with external knowledge, yet existing graph-based methods face a fundamental limitation: entity-centric and chunk-centric approaches operate on representations anchored to original text without true knowledge fusion. While entity-centric methods connect logically related content and chunk-centric methods preserve context, both retrieve information separately through similarity search, missing emergent understanding from their synthesis. In this paper, we propose HyGRAG, a hierarchical graph RAG framework that transcends source documents by addressing three core challenges: constructing summaries that genuinely integrate contextual and relational information, leveraging these synthesized representations to access emergent knowledge during retrieval, and efficiently updating hierarchical structures for dynamic corpora. Specifically, we design hierarchical index structures over hybrid graphs with both chunk and entity nodes, then iteratively cluster them and generate LLM-based summaries. Then, we design context and relation-aware retrieval that searches across all abstraction levels while expanding through community membership. Moreover, we enable dynamic knowledge update through attachment-based algorithms with only local re-summarization. Experimental results show that HyGRAG improves the average accuracy of multi-hop reasoning tasks by 9.7%, while maintaining reasonable efficiency.

</details>


### [162] [IsabeLLM: Automated Theorem Proving Applied to Formally Verifying Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18098)
*Elliot Jones,William Knottenbelt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文改进了Isabelle定理证明工具IsabeLLM，通过引入检索增强生成框架、错误追踪和反例生成技术，验证了比特币工作量证明共识协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链系统频繁遭受恶意攻击导致巨额经济损失，亟需更好的验证方法。共识协议是系统最关键组件，而传统形式验证需要大量专业知识和投入。AI定理证明技术可以自动化大量工作负载，使形式验证更加普及。

Method: 在IsabeLLM基础上实现了三大改进：1）检索增强生成(RAG)框架；2）错误追踪机制；3）反例生成功能，为大型语言模型提供更好的上下文。同时实现了与最新版本Isabelle和Sledgehammer的兼容性以提高效率。

Result: 对比了新旧两个版本IsabeLLM在完成比特币工作量证明共识验证任务中的性能表现，验证了改进方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 改进后的IsabeLLM能够更有效地自动化区块链共识协议的形式验证，为区块链系统安全性验证提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Advances in Artificial Intelligence (AI) have led AI for Theorem Proving to become a promising means of formally verifying computer systems. Whilst formal verification is traditionally reserved for safety-critical systems due to the required amount of expertise and effort, AI can help to automate a large amount of this workload and make it far more accessible. Blockchain-based systems are becoming increasingly popular and are frequently targeted by malicious actors, often resulting in huge financial losses, highlighting the need to better verify these systems and mitigate vulnerabilities. Arguably the most important component of these systems is the consensus protocol, which allows nodes to agree on decisions in a potentially adversarial environment. In this paper, we improve upon IsabeLLM, the automated theorem proving tool in Isabelle. Namely, we implement a Retrieval-Augmented Generation framework, Error tracing and counterexample generation for improved context supplied to the Large Language Model. Compatibility with the latest version of Isabelle and Sledgehammer is also implemented for improved efficiency. We compare the performance of the two versions of IsabeLLM in their ability to complete the verification of Bitcoin's Proof of Work consensus.

</details>


### [163] [Trust the Right Teacher: Quality-Aware Self-Distillation for GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18101)
*Jingyuan Huang,Zuming Huang,Yucheng Shi,Tianze Yang,Xiaoming Zhai,Wei Chu,Ninghao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对GUI定位任务，提出质量感知自蒸馏方法，通过软正确性门控和教师概率缩放的互补结合，解决了传统在线策略自蒸馏中坐标token教师信号质量退化的问题，显著提升了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI定位需要视觉语言模型在高分辨率截图中识别小目标元素并预测精确坐标。在线策略自蒸馏(OPSD)虽能提供密集token级监督，但在学生生成的前缀偏离目标坐标时，坐标token的教师信号质量会退化，导致监督不可靠。

Method: 提出质量感知自蒸馏方法，包含两个核心组件：(1)软正确性感知门控：检查教师当前的坐标token预测是否能在学生生成前缀下完成真实框，若不能则降低对应教师信号权重；(2)教师概率缩放：利用教师置信度作为轻量级因子进一步校准门控监督强度。实验发现两者单独使用无效，必须结合才能互补发挥作用。

Result: 在六个GUI定位基准上的实验表明，该方法持续改进基础模型性能并超越强基线方法。关键发现是两个组件单独使用均无法提升性能，结合后才能持续改进，证明两者具有互补作用。

Conclusion: 质量感知自蒸馏通过正确性门控抑制不可靠的坐标token监督，教师概率缩放校准剩余信号强度，两种机制协同作用有效解决了GUI定位中坐标敏感任务的监督质量问题，为VLM的GUI应用提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) grounding requires vision-language models (VLMs) to identify small target elements in high-resolution screenshots and predict precise screen coordinates. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) is a promising post-training approach for this coordinate-sensitive task, since it provides dense token-level teacher signals beyond hard coordinate labels. However, naive OPSD is not well suited to GUI grounding: OPSD evaluates the teacher on student-generated prefixes, the quality of coordinate-token teacher signals can degrade when the prefix has already deviated from the target coordinate, leading to unreliable teacher signal. To mitigate this, We propose quality-aware self-distillation for VLM-based GUI grounding, which improves coordinate-token teacher-signal quality through soft correctness-aware gating and teacher-probability scaling. The soft correctness-aware gate checks whether the teacher's current coordinate-token prediction can still be completed into the ground-truth box under the student-generated prefix. If not, the corresponding teacher signal is down-weighted. Teacher-probability scaling then uses the teacher's confidence as a lightweight factor to further calibrate the strength of the gated supervision. A key empirical finding is that neither component alone improves overall performance, whereas combining them consistently improves performance. This suggests that the two mechanisms play complementary roles: correctness-aware gating suppresses unreliable coordinate-token supervision, while teacher-probability scaling calibrates the strength of the remaining signals. Experiments across six GUI grounding benchmarks show that our method consistently improves the base model and outperforms strong baselines.

</details>


### [164] [First Proof Second Batch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18119)
*Mohammed Abouzaid,Nikhil Srivastava,Rachel Ward,Lauren Williams*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了多个AI系统在十个研究级数学问题上的表现，涵盖广泛的数学领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了评估当前AI系统正确解决研究级数学问题的能力，作者们设计了一套测试方案。

Method: 研究者们从多位数学家的研究过程中收集了十个自然产生的研究级数学问题，用这些问题对多个AI系统进行测试，并收集了人类解法、AI生成解法以及评审报告和日志作为补充材料。

Result: 文档详细记录了这十个问题的内容、测试方法论以及AI系统的测试结果，并通过补充材料提供了人类解法与AI解法的对比分析。

Conclusion: 该研究为评估AI在研究级数学问题上的能力提供了系统的基准测试方法和实证数据。

Abstract: To assess the ability of current AI systems to correctly solve research-level mathematics problems, we tested several AI systems on a set of ten problems in a broad range of mathematical fields; these problems arose naturally in the research process of the contributors. This document includes the problems, our methodology, and the results of our testing. We provide links to supplementary documents including the human solutions, the AI-generated solutions, and the referee reports and logs for the AI-generated solutions.
  The ten problems were contributed by the following mathematicians: (1) Dariusz Kalociński and Theodore A. Slaman, (2) Richard Schwartz, (3) Aleksa Milojevic and Benny Sudakov, (4) Larry Guth, (5) Oleg Butkovsky, Jonathan Mattingly, and Lorenzo Zambotti, (6) Joshua Evan Greene and Duncan McCoy, (7) Sucharit Sarkar, (8) Sam Payne and Jidong (Jayden) Wang, (9) Sylvie Corteel and John Lentfer, (10) Srivatsav Kunnawalkam Elayavalli.

</details>


### [165] [Knowledge Reutilization in Meta-Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18132)
*Yuan Meng,Bo Wang,Juan de los Rios Ruiz,Xiangtong Yao,Zhenshan Bing,Fuchun Sun,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出元知识复用框架，通过在动力学简化的智能体上学习任务级知识并迁移至异构智能体，显著降低了追踪误差（94.75%—99.79%）并以约23.8%的交互数据达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端元强化学习方法将任务推断与特定具身控制耦合，导致非参数任务语义模糊、样本效率降低且跨智能体重用受限。

Method: 在动力学简化的智能体上学习任务级知识；使用贝叶斯非参数先验组织潜在任务模式；高层策略生成任务级幅度指导；通过语义-幅度接口和轻量时序适配器将冻结的元知识转换为时序对齐的子目标，供具身特定的低层控制器使用。

Result: 在多种运动智能体上，最终步追踪误差相比近期SOTA基线降低94.75%—99.79%；仅需约23.8%的交互数据即可达到相当的部署性能。

Conclusion: 解耦任务推断与具身控制的元知识复用框架能有效提升样本效率与跨智能体迁移能力，为异构智能体的快速适应提供新范式。

Abstract: Meta-reinforcement learning enables fast adaptation by extracting shared structure from related tasks, but existing end-to-end methods often couple task inference with embodiment-specific control. This coupling can obscure non-parametric task semantics, reduce sample efficiency, and limit cross-agent reuse. We propose a meta-knowledge reutilization framework that learns task-level knowledge on a dynamics-simplified agent and transfers it to heterogeneous agents. The framework uses a Bayesian non-parametric prior to organize latent task modes and a high-level policy to generate task-level magnitude guidance. To bridge reusable task knowledge with different embodiments, we introduce a semantic-magnitude interface and a lightweight temporal adaptor, which convert frozen meta-knowledge into temporally aligned subgoals for embodiment-specific low-level controllers. Experiments on multiple locomotion agents show that our framework reduces final-step tracking error by 94.75% -- 99.79% compared with recent state-of-the-art baselines and achieves comparable deployment performance with about 23.8% of their interaction data.

</details>


### [166] [Your AI Travel Agent Would Book You a Bullfight: An Agentic Benchmark for Implicit Animal Welfare in Frontier AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18142)
*Jasmine Brazilek,Oliver Tulio,Joel Christoph,Miles Tidmarsh,Carol Kline,Arturs Kanepajs*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了TAC（旅行代理同情心），这是首个用于评估AI智能体在代表用户行动时是否避免涉及动物剥削选项的基准测试。七个前沿模型的表现均低于随机水平（64%），最佳模型Claude Opus 4.7仅达53%。在系统提示中加入一句关注动物福利的句子可显著提升Claude和GPT-5.5的表现47-63个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI和动物福利基准主要评估模型对问答提示的文本响应，但尚不清楚这些响应中呈现的福利推理是否能够迁移到模型需要使用工具采取行动的智能体部署场景。随着AI智能体从顾问角色转向代表用户执行实际操作（如预订旅行、规划菜单、运行采购），评估其在行动中的伦理决策变得至关重要。

Method: 研究团队开发了TAC基准，包含12个人工编写的旅行预订场景，涵盖六类动物剥削类别，并通过控制价格、评分和位置混淆因素扩展至48个样本。研究者评估了来自四个实验室的七个前沿模型，并通过在系统提示中加入一句福利意识语句进行干预实验。此外，使用Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite作为评判者，对表现最佳的两个模型的288个基础条件转录进行辅助审计，以检测评估意识。

Result: 所有模型得分均低于64%的随机水平，最佳表现者Claude Opus 4.7仅达53%。在系统提示中加入一句福利意识语句后，Claude和GPT-5.5的提升幅度达47-63个百分点，GPT-5.2提升26个百分点，而DeepSeek和Gemini的提升不足12个百分点。辅助审计未发现任何评估意识标记，表明低于随机的表现并非源于模型识别出评估性质。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了文本响应型福利基准的局限性，以及AI智能体在实际行动场景中的伦理决策缺陷。结果对跨文化领域的类别级变异、欧盟通用人工智能行为准则中的系统性风险框架具有重要启示，强调了在智能体部署中需要更有效的福利保障机制。

Abstract: AI agents are moving from advisors to actors, booking travel, planning menus, and running procurement on behalf of users. Existing benchmarks for AI and animal welfare evaluate model text responses to question-answer prompts, leaving open whether the welfare reasoning surfaced in those responses transfers to agentic deployment where the model must take actions with tools. We introduce TAC (Travel Agent Compassion), the first agentic benchmark measuring whether AI agents avoid options involving animal exploitation when acting on behalf of users. TAC presents an AI agent with twelve hand-authored travel booking scenarios across six categories of animal exploitation, augmented to forty-eight samples to control for price, rating, and position confounds. We evaluate seven frontier models from four labs. Every model scores below the chance level of sixty-four percent, with the best performer (Claude Opus 4.7) at fifty-three percent. A single welfare-aware sentence in the system prompt yields gains of forty-seven to sixty-three percentage points in Claude and GPT-5.5, twenty-six points in GPT-5.2, and under twelve points in DeepSeek and Gemini. An auxiliary Inspect Scout audit of 288 base-condition transcripts from the top two performers, using Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite as judge, flags zero transcripts for evaluation awareness, suggesting the below-chance rates do not stem from the models recognising the evaluation. We discuss implications for category-level variation across cultural domains, the limits of text-response welfare benchmarks, and the EU General-Purpose AI Code of Practice systemic risk framework.

</details>


### [167] [Memory as a Wasting Asset: Pricing Flash Endurance for Embodied Agents, and the Limits of Doing So](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18144)
*Josef Liyanjun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将机器人闪存耐久性视为折旧资本的方法，通过单一耐久性影子价格η对内存写入进行定价，在RAM/板载NVM/云存储层级中实现成本最优的数据放置策略。实证表明价值-写入关联性χ的符号决定了最优策略是否非单调：在长期操纵任务中为正、短视任务中为零、非循环遥操作中为负。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人的闪存耐久性是不可再生的存量资源，每次持久化写入都会消耗有限的程序/擦除周期（仅几千次），但现有的机器人内存系统都没有对哪些内存值得消耗擦除周期进行定价。这一资源稀缺性在低成本边缘机器人使用的QLC/eMMC存储（约1000次P/E周期）上尤为突出。

Method: 将具身内存视为折旧资本，引入单一耐久性影子价格η进行定价；构建磨损增强的每字节索引，将成本最小化的放置策略转化为阈值决策；在RAM/板载NVM/云存储三层架构中实现最优放置；通过真实机器人日志数据实证测量价值-写入关联性χ的符号。

Result: 价值-写入关联性χ的符号是部署机制的属性：在循环长期操纵任务中为正（χ̂≈+1.0×10⁻³）、短视任务中为零、非循环遥操作中为负。只有当χ>0时，最优策略呈现非单调性，将机器人最有价值的内存送离闪存。耐久性预算在高端TLC存储（3000次P/E）上不构成约束，但在廉价QLC/eMMC上成为瓶颈。基于学习的磨损感知控制器与基于价格的路由策略在任务价值上表现相当，因为实现的价值在各存储层级间不变。

Conclusion: 本文证明了基于磨损感知的内存放置策略的理论最优性，并通过实证验证了不同部署场景下价值-写入关联性的差异。该方法在廉价边缘机器人的存储资源受限场景中具有实际应用价值。然而，磨损感知放置是否真正提升任务价值仍是开放问题——χ是相对于价值代理测量的，非单调最优策略虽已证明但尚未在数据中观察到。

Abstract: A robot's flash endurance is a non-renewable stock: every persisted write spends one of a few thousand program/erase cycles and never refills, yet no fielded robot memory system prices which memories are worth an erase cycle. We treat embodied memory as depreciating capital and price that stock with a single endurance shadow price $η$, which makes cost-minimizing placement across a RAM / on-board NVM / cloud hierarchy a threshold in a wear-augmented per-byte index. The index is cost-optimal whatever the sign of the value-write association $χ$; only when $χ> 0$ does the optimum turn non-monotone, sending a robot's most valuable memories off its flash.
  The pivot is thus empirical, and we measure $χ$ on real robot logs at a pre-specified gate: its sign is a property of the deployment regime -- positive on recurrent long-horizon manipulation ($\hatχ \approx +1.0 \times 10^{-3}$, replicated at full power), null on a shorter-horizon suite, and negative on non-recurrent teleoperation. Two boundaries scope the result. The endurance budget is dormant on premium 3,000-P/E TLC at datasheet prices and binding on the commodity QLC/eMMC ($\sim$1,000 P/E) that cheaper edge robots run. And where it binds, a learned wear-aware controller only ties price-based routing on task value, because realized value is tier-invariant across RAM, NVM, and cloud: the rent governs device lifetime and cost, not task performance. Whether wear-aware placement improves task value remains open -- $χ$ is measured against a value proxy, and the non-monotone optimum, while proven, is not yet observed in data.

</details>


### [168] [WEQA: Wearable hEalth Question Answering with Query-Adaptive Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18147)
*Yuwei Zhang,Tong Xia,Bianca Emmerich,Yu Yvonne Wu,Dimitris Spathis,Xin Liu,Daniel McDuff,Cecilia Mascolo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了WEQA框架，通过LLM控制器动态协调专业传感器分析工具，解决了可穿戴健康数据问答的挑战，准确率相比基线提升24%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管语言模型在医学问答上表现优异，但在处理可穿戴健康数据方面仍存在挑战。可穿戴传感器产生的数据具有连续性、高维度和纵向性特点，难以与LLM预训练的文本中心分布对齐，且固定的推理流程或单一基础模型无法有效处理传感器模态和用户意图的多样性。

Method: 提出WEQA（查询自适应代理框架），采用LLLM控制器来综合执行计划，动态将每个查询路由到适当的传感器分析和预训练模型组合中，并利用外部知识进行基于事实的响应审计。该框架将LLM推理与专业的可穿戴分析建模工具相统一。

Result: 在包含四个开放可穿戴数据集、跨越三个健康领域的分析及预测任务基准上，WEQA框架比LLM和代理基线准确率提高24%；与12位医学专家和8位用户的盲测研究显示，该框架在有用性和临床合理性方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: WEQA框架有效解决了可穿戴健康数据问答的挑战，通过自适应地协调专业工具与LLLM推理，显著提升了系统的准确性、有用性和临床可靠性，为可穿戴健康数据分析提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Language models are remarkably capable at medical question answering, in some cases surpassing the accuracy of general physicians. However, answering questions about wearable health data remains challenging and understudied, as these ubiquitous sensors produce continuous, high-dimensional, and longitudinal data, which is non-trivial to align with text-centric distributions in LLM pretraining. The diversity of sensor modalities and user intents cannot be effectively handled by a fixed reasoning workflow or a single pretrained foundation model. To address these challenges, we propose WEQA, a query-adaptive agent framework that unifies LLM reasoning with specialized wearable analytical and modeling tools. An LLM controller is employed to synthesize execution plans and dynamically route each query to the appropriate combination of sensor analysis and pretrained models, and perform grounded response auditing with external knowledge. We also curate a benchmark spanning four open wearable datasets comprising analytic and predictive tasks in three different health domains. Experiments show that our framework is 24% more accurate than LLM and agentic baselines, and a blinded study with 12 medical experts and 8 users shows substantial gains in usefulness and clinical soundness.

</details>


### [169] [Learning Cardiac Electrophysiology Digital Twins Through Agentic Discovery of Hybrid Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18154)
*Ziqi Zhou,Yubo Ye,Sumeet Atul Vadhavka,Linwei Wang,Zhiqiang Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LEADS框架，利用LLM智能体发现物理约束的混合模型，实现个性化心脏电生理数字孪生的自动架构设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建个性化心脏电生理数字孪生需要为每位患者识别合适的模型结构，而非仅拟合参数。传统方法依赖专家手动设计混合物理-神经架构，需要深厚领域知识且无法跨患者迁移；现有基于LLM的方法虽具泛化能力，但缺乏心脏模拟稳定性所需的结构先验。

Method: LEADS将心脏电生理领域知识形式化为结构化动作空间，采用LLM智能体通过迭代推理-行动循环选择、组合和精炼混合模型，梯度下降负责参数拟合。每个候选模型确保物理基础、可解释性和数值稳定性，同时允许开放式架构探索。

Result: 在三种ground truth反应模型的合成数据和真实心脏电生理数据上验证，LEADS性能优于人工设计的混合模型及其他基于LLM的混合建模方法。

Conclusion: LEADS成功实现了领域知识引导下的自动化混合模型发现，为个性化心脏电生理数字孪生提供了一种有效且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Building personalized cardiac electrophysiology (EP) digital twins requires identifying the appropriate model structure for each patient, not merely fitting parameters. Traditional methods rely on experts to manually prescribe hybrid physics-neural architectures, which requires deep domain expertise and does not transfer across patients. Recent works have applied large language models (LLMs) to generate or act as hybrid models. However, despite their promising generalization capacity, these LLM-based methods lack the structural priors needed for stable cardiac simulations. Hence, we propose LEADS, a framework that formulates cardiac EP domain knowledge as a structured action space and utilizes an LLM agent to discover hybrid models. The agent follows an iterative reasoning-and-action loop to select, combine, and refine hybrid models, whilst gradient descent handles parameter fitting. The proposed LEADS designs every candidate model towards physically grounded, interpretable, and numerically stable, while allowing open-ended architectural discovery. We validate LEADS on synthetic data with three ground-truth reaction models and on real cardiac EP data, demonstrating that it outperforms both human-designed hybrid models and other LLM-based hybrid modeling.

</details>


### [170] [The Stanford EDGAR Filings Dataset: Reconstructing U.S. Corporate and Financial Disclosures into Layout-Faithful and Token-Efficient Pretraining Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18192)
*Nick Bettencourt,Xiaowei Ding,Kay Giesecke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发布了SEFD数据集，将SEC文件重建为布局保真的MultiMarkdown格式，提供152B token的高质量金融长文本预训练语料，并推出两个评估基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量公共网络语料库日益枯竭，清洁的长上下文文档成为LLM训练数据的稀缺且昂贵的来源。现有长文本语料库通常为专有资源、合成生成或集中在编程等狭窄领域，缺乏开放、高质量的长文本金融数据资源。

Method: 将SEC文件重建为布局保真的MultiMarkdown格式，构建Stanford EDGAR Filings Dataset (SEFD)。该数据集包含审计财务报表、风险披露、所有权报告、会计附注和市场重大事件文件，可作为长文本预训练数据，支持金融推理、预测、合规和文档理解任务。

Result: 发布了SEFD-v1初始公共快照（152B token），并对更大的1850万份文件档案（约550B token）进行语料库级分析。该语料库与Common Crawl衍生语料库重叠率低于0.1%，具有token高效、模型就绪的特点。同时推出了两个基准测试：EDGAR-Forecast（评估基于文件的数值预测）和EDGAR-OCR（评估复杂金融表格转录）。

Conclusion: SEFD为LLM提供了高质量、开放获取的金融长文本预训练数据，填补了长上下文金融语料库的空白，并通过配套基准测试推动了金融语言建模和评估领域的发展。

Abstract: As high-quality public web corpora become increasingly exhausted, clean long-context documents have become a scarce and expensive source of training data for large language models (LLMs). Existing long-context corpora are often proprietary and costly to acquire, synthetically generated, or concentrated in narrow domains such as programming. We introduce the Stanford EDGAR Filings Dataset (SEFD), an open reconstruction of SEC filings into layout-faithful MultiMarkdown for financial language modeling and evaluation. SEFD makes audited financial statements, risk disclosures, ownership reports, accounting notes, and market-moving event filings usable as long-context pretraining data and as a basis for financial reasoning, forecasting, compliance, and document understanding. The resulting corpus is token-efficient, model-ready, and has less than 0.1% overlap with Common Crawl-derived corpora. We release SEFD-v1, a 152B-token initial public snapshot, and provide corpus-level analyses of a larger 18.5M-filing archive estimated at 550B tokens. We further introduce two SEFD-derived benchmarks: EDGAR-Forecast, which evaluates filing-grounded numerical forecasting after model knowledge cutoffs, and EDGAR-OCR, which evaluates transcription of complex financial tables.

</details>


### [171] [Fixed-Point Reasoners: Stable and Adaptive Deep Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18206)
*Sajad Movahedi,Vera Milovanović,Shlomo Libo Feigin,Alexander Theus,Thomas Hofmann,Valentina Boeva,T. Konstantin Rusch,Antonio Orvieto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了FPRM（基于Transformer的定点推理模型），通过使用预归一化层和残差缩放解决循环架构中的信号传播问题，并利用定点收敛作为端到端的停止机制，使模型能够根据任务难度自适应调整计算量，在Sudoku、迷宫、状态跟踪和ARC-AGI等推理基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环架构在需要组合推理的任务中具有学习逐步过程的归纳偏置优势，但类似于深度架构，当推迟停止决策时，循环架构会受到深度引起的信号传播问题影响，导致模型性能下降。同时，模型需要能够根据任务复杂度自适应调整计算资源。

Method: 提出了FPRM模型，采用两个关键技术：(1) 使用预归一化层和残差缩放解决信号传播问题；(2) 在循环架构中使用定点收敛作为端到端的停止机制，实现自适应计算。该模型基于Transformer架构构建。

Result: FPRM能够根据任务难度自适应调整计算量，在常见的推理基准测试上表现有效，包括Sudoku、迷宫(Maze)、状态跟踪(state-tracking)和ARC-AGI等任务。

Conclusion: 通过解决循环架构中的信号传播问题并引入定点收敛停止机制，FPRM成功实现了自适应计算，在多个推理基准测试上展现出优异性能，证明了该方法在组合推理任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Looped architectures provide an inductive bias toward learning step-by-step procedures for tasks that require compositional reasoning. The number of effective layers reached by looping determines the quality of the solution these models find. Like deep architectures, looped architectures are prone to a signal propagation problem induced by depth as the halting decision is postponed. In this paper, we address this signal propagation issue using pre-norm layers and residual scaling. Building on these architectural modifications, we propose FPRM, a Transformer-based Fixed-Point Reasoning Model that uses fixed-point convergence as an end-to-end halting mechanism in a looped architecture. We show that fixed-point halting allows FPRM to adapt its compute to task difficulty. FPRM is effective on common reasoning benchmarks, namely Sudoku, Maze, state-tracking, and ARC-AGI.

</details>


### [172] [EvolveNav: Proactive Preflection and Self-Evolving Memory for Zero-Shot Object Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18235)
*Qi Chai,Wenhao Shen,Nanjie Yao,Yue Xia,Kaiyong Zhao,Jie Ma,Guosheng Lin,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自演化的零样本目标导航框架，通过构建智能体规则记忆和记忆引导的预反思模块，在测试时持续改进性能，成功率达到10.1%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的零样本目标导航方法依赖基础模型的静态先验知识，缺乏适应性，导致重复错误和低效的试错探索。

Method: 构建智能体规则记忆以从历史轨迹中提取可操作知识；提出基于置信区间的检索策略，平衡语义相关性和历史成功率选择有效规则；引入记忆引导的预反思模块，在行动前预测潜在结果以减少低效探索。

Result: 在广泛实验中，该方法优于现有零样本基线，成功率提升10.1%，且减少了不必要的步骤。

Conclusion: 所提出的自演化框架能够实现测试时的持续改进，有效解决了静态先验导致的重复错误问题，为零样本目标导航提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Zero-Shot Object-Goal Navigation (ZS-OGN) requires embodied agents to explore and locate target objects without any prior training. To this end, recent methods leverage foundation models. But they typically rely on static priors and lack adaptation, which leads to repeated errors and costly trial and error. In this paper, we propose a self-evolving ZS-OGN framework that enables continuous test-time improvement. Specifically, we build an agentic rule memory by extracting actionable knowledge from past trajectories. Then, we propose a retrieval strategy based on upper confidence bound, selecting effective rules by balancing semantic relevance and historical success. In addition, we introduce a memory-guided preflection module that forecasts potential outcomes before action, reducing inefficient exploration. Extensive experiments show that our method outperforms existing zero-shot baselines, achieving a 10.1\% improvement in success rate with fewer unnecessary steps.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [173] [Cordon: Semantic Transactions for Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17573)
*Zheng Chen,Hanqing Liu,Duling Xu,Dong Dong,Jialin Li,Bangzheng Pu,Jidong Zhai*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文提出了Cordon，一个事务性运行时系统，通过语义事务为工具使用的LLM代理提供任务级执行边界，解决了现有运行时缺乏跨多步骤工作流的提交、回滚、恢复和审计机制的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前工具使用的LLM代理运行时将工具暴露为孤立的RPC调用，这种接口虽然便于集成，但缺乏任务范围执行边界，无法支持多步骤代理工作流的提交、回滚、恢复和审计，导致模型驱动的有状态任务存在安全风险。

Method: 引入语义事务概念作为任务级执行边界，绑定工具意图和运行时追踪的结果谱系到可逆的本地状态、分阶段外部效果、委托权限和审计元数据。Cordon实现了事务管理器，追踪派生结果对象，在影子状态中执行可逆变更，在外部效果发件箱中暂存对外操作，记录恢复元数据，并在提交状态或释放外部效果前验证组合执行流程。

Result: 评估结果表明，Cordon能够发现现有防御机制遗漏的跨步骤违规行为，减少不可逆效果故障，同时保持良性任务完成率，且仅带来适度的审批和延迟开销。

Conclusion: 运行时容器边界而非单次调用护栏是解决LLM代理执行安全问题的正确方向，Cordon通过事务性运行时系统成功实现了跨步骤的安全控制和验证。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents are shifting the unit of computation from explicit human-issued commands to model-driven tasks with stateful consequences. Yet today's agent runtimes still expose tools as isolated RPCs. This interface gives runtimes a convenient integration point, but it lacks a task-scoped execution boundary for commit, rollback, recovery, and audit across multi-step agent workflows. We argue that this mismatch calls for a runtime containment boundary rather than another per-call guardrail. This paper introduces Cordon, a transactional runtime system for staging and validating irreversible agent effects before commit. A semantic transaction is a task-level execution boundary that binds tool intents and runtime-tracked result lineage to reversible local state, staged external effects, delegated authority, and audit metadata. Cordon implements this abstraction with a transaction manager that tracks derived result objects, executes reversible mutations in shadow state, stages outward-facing actions in an effect outbox, and records recovery metadata. The runtime then validates the composed execution flow before it commits state or releases external effects. Our evaluation across adversarial and benign workflows shows that Cordon exposes cross-step violations missed by existing defenses. It also reduces irreversible-effect failures while preserving benign task completion with modest approval and latency overhead.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [RATIO: Redundancy-Controlled Stochastic Routing for Reliable Vehicular Multi-Hop Networking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17481)
*Lei Lei,Xudong Wang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了RATIO路由方案，通过概率转发机制实现冗余度的连续可控，有效平衡了车载网络中多跳数据传输的可靠性与延迟权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车载网络中高移动性和间歇性阻塞导致频繁路由失效，现有确定性多路径复制方案将数据包复制到整数数量的分支，难以灵活调整冗余级别以适应时变的网络动态，而过度的冗余复制又会加剧竞争并引入额外延迟。

Method: 提出冗余可控随机路由（RATIO）：为每个活跃流构建加权简化有向无环图（DAG）作为路由结构，边权重指定每条链路的转发概率；在分叉节点采用基于模运算的随机转发规则，允许聚合转发概率超过1，实现冗余度的连续控制；进一步提出启发式算法H-RATIO，通过候选路径集合并优化转发概率。

Result: 基于SUMO/ns-3联合仿真的大量实验表明，RATIO/H-RATIO在及时投递率（PDR）方面始终优于基线算法，同时在高负载场景下显著提升了投递效率。

Conclusion: RATIO路由方案通过概率转发机制实现了冗余度的连续可控调节，在保证高可靠低延迟传输的同时有效避免了过度复制带来的问题，为车载网络中的多跳数据传输提供了一种高效解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable, low-latency multi-hop data delivery in vehicular networks is increasingly demanded, yet remains challenging due to frequent route failures caused by high mobility and intermittent blockage. While redundancy-based routing enhances robustness by forwarding packets over multiple paths, over-replication intensifies contention and introduces additional delay, highlighting the need to carefully managing redundancy--reliability trade-off. However, conventional deterministic multi-path replication typically duplicates packets to an integer number of branches, making the redundancy level hard to tune and adapt to time-varying network dynamics in vehicular networks. To this end, Redundancy-Controlled Stochastic (RATIO) routing is proposed in this paper. For each active flow, RATIO constructs a weighted reduced directed acyclic graph (DAG) as the routing structure, where edge weights specify per-link forwarding probabilities. At fork nodes, the aggregate outgoing forwarding probability is allowed to exceed one and a modulo-based stochastic forwarding rule is employed to guarantee feasible forwarding, thereby enabling continuously controllable redundancy. An idealized RATIO design is formulated as a load-minimizing optimization subject to per-flow timely-reliability and link-capacity constraints, but the problem is generally intractable under time-varying wireless dynamics. Accordingly, a practical heuristic, termed H-RATIO, is developed. H-RATIO constructs a compact reduced DAG by taking the union of candidate paths and optimizes forwarding probabilities via local scoring and replication-adjustment iterations. Extensive trace-driven SUMO/ns-3 co-simulations demonstrate that RATIO/H-RATIO consistently achieves the highest timely PDR compared to baselines, while providing substantially better delivery efficiency, especially under high-load scenarios.

</details>


### [175] [Predictive Configured Grant Scheduling for Deterministic Wireless Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17653)
*Syed Morsleen Riaz,M. Carmen Lucas-Estañ,Baldomero Coll-Perales,Javier Gozalvez*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种预测性配置授权(CG)调度方案，通过基于流量预测预分配资源并考虑预测误差，在可变流量模式下显著提升确定性服务水平的支持能力和资源利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来无线网络需要增强容量以维持确定性服务水平并支持新兴的时间敏感服务。保证有界时延高度依赖于高效的无线资源管理。现有配置授权(CG)调度虽然可以通过预分配资源降低时延，但在可变流量模式下其有效性和效率会下降。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的预测性CG调度方案，该方案基于流量预测预分配资源，同时考虑了预测的不准确性。通过将预测误差纳入考虑，该方案能够更好地满足有界时延要求。

Result: 评估结果表明，所提出的方案显著增强了支持确定性服务水平的容量，同时提高了资源利用率，即使在不同需求和混合流量的可变场景中也能保持良好性能。

Conclusion: 考虑预测不准确性的预测性CG调度方案能够有效提升无线网络支持确定性服务的能力，为时间敏感服务提供了更可靠的保障。

Abstract: Future wireless networks must enhance their capacity to sustain deterministic service levels and support emerging time-sensitive services in key verticals. The ability to guarantee bounded latencies heavily depends on efficient radio resource management. Configured Grant (CG) scheduling can reduce latency by pre-allocating resources, but its effectiveness and efficiency decrease under variable traffic patterns. This study presents a novel predictive CG scheduling scheme that pre-allocates resources based on traffic predictions while accounting for prediction inaccuracies. By considering these inaccuracies, the scheme significantly improves the ability to meet bounded latency requirements, which are essential for supporting deterministic service levels. Our evaluations show that the proposed scheme significantly enhances the capacity to support deterministic service levels while improving resource utilization, even in scenarios with variable and mixed traffic flows with diverse requirements.

</details>


### [176] [5G Network Architecture and Configuration Choices to Support Teleoperated Driving at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17654)
*M. C. Lucas-Estañ,B. Coll-Perales,M. I. Khan,J. Gozálvez,S. S. Avedisov,O. Altintas,M. Sepulcre*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文对比分析了不同5G网络架构对多车远程驾驶（ToD）服务的支持能力，发现MEC/边缘5G网络比集中式网络更适合ToD服务的扩展，并通过量化分析提出了优化网络配置的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程驾驶需要车辆向控制中心传输视频流，对上行带宽、低延迟和可靠性有严格要求。虽然5G网络具备这些能力，但当前网络主要针对移动宽带业务配置，尚不清楚现有5G架构是否能有效支持多车同时远程驾驶的巨大上行带宽需求。

Method: 研究量化分析了在不同5G网络架构（集中式 vs MEC/边缘）和配置（包括不同双工模式和TDD帧结构）下，同时远程驾驶多辆车辆所需的带宽；并研究了控制信道配置如何缓解视频流处理时间对服务扩展的影响。

Result: MEC/边缘式5G网络在支持和扩展ToD服务方面优于集中式网络；明确了不同网络架构和配置下的多车远程驾驶带宽需求；证明控制信道的合理配置能够缓解视频处理时间对ToD服务容量的影响。

Conclusion: 为支持多车远程驾驶服务的规模化部署，应采用MEC/边缘式5G网络架构，并优化控制信道配置以提升系统容量，这为ToD服务的实际部署提供了重要的网络架构和配置指导。

Abstract: Teleoperated driving (ToD) enables the remote driving or control of vehicles. For this purpose, vehicles must transmit video feeds to the ToD control center so that the remote operator is fully aware of the driving conditions and can safely control the vehicle. 5G (and beyond) networks are fundamental for the deployment of ToD as they can provide the low latency, reliable and broadband connection necessary to connect the vehicle and ToD control center. However, it is unclear whether common 5G network architectures and configurations are well-suited to support the simultaneous teleoperation of multiple vehicles with demanding uplink bandwidth, as current networks are mainly configured to support mobile broadband services. This paper demonstrates that MEC or edge-based 5G networks are better suited to support and scale the ToD service than centralized networks, and quantifies the bandwidth required to simultaneously teleoperate multiple vehicles under various 5G network architectures and configurations, including different duplexing modes and TDD frame structures. Finally, the study shows that the configuration of the control channels can help mitigate the impact that the processing time of the video feeds has on the capacity to support and scale the ToD service.

</details>


### [177] [Integration of 5G and Industrial Digital Models: A Case Study with AGVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17655)
*J. Cañete-Martín,J. Gómez-Jerez,M. C. Lucas-Estañ,J. Gozálvez*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文首次实现了工业数字模型与5G数字模型的集成（基于资产管理壳AAS），通过AGV案例证明了集成模型可量化和评估5G通信对工业过程的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能制造依赖数字模型优化工业流程，但现有模型未考虑5G通信影响，无法充分设计和优化基于5G的工业过程，需要将5G模型与工业数字模型进行集成。

Method: 将5G系统实现为资产管理壳（AAS）形式的数字模型，通过OPC UA接口将5G数字模型与工业数字模型互联，以AGV物料运输为案例进行验证。

Result: 证明了通过集成5G和工业数字模型，可以量化和评估5G通信对工业过程运行和生产效率的影响，为联合设计和优化提供了基础。

Conclusion: 将5G集成到工业数字模型中对实现两者的联合设计和优化具有重要性和必要性。

Abstract: 5G is a fundamental technology for the digitalization of smart manufacturing. Smart manufacturing relies on the use of digital models to optimize industrial processes before implementation on the manufacturing plants. These models should account for the impact of 5G communications to adequately dimension and optimize 5G-based industrial processes. This paper presents the first integration of industrial digital models with a 5G digital model, implemented as an Asset Administration Shell (AAS) of a 5G system. The two models are interconnected using an OPC UA-based interface. We evaluate the impact of the integrated model using a use case where Automated Guided Vehicles (AGVs) transport material from a warehouse to production lines. The AGVs periodically exchange their positions over 5G to avoid potential collisions. If the communications fail, the AGVs stop for safety reasons until a reliable 5G connection can be guaranteed. We demonstrate that, by integrating 5G and industrial digital models, it is possible to account for, and quantify, the impact of 5G communications on the operation and productivity of industrial processes. This result highlights the importance and necessity of integrating 5G into industrial digital models for their joint design and optimization.

</details>


### [178] [DPDS: A DPDK-Based Packet Delayer and Spacer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17716)
*Etienne Zink,Fabian Ihle,Michael Menth*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应延迟相关性方法，解决链路仿真中添加变化延迟时的包乱序问题，并实现基于DPDK的高性能包延迟器DPDS，在零丢包情况下达到95 Gbit/s吞吐量，显著优于现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在链路仿真中，为数据包添加变化延迟时，简单方法要么导致实际延迟超过期望值，要么引起数据包乱序，这两种结果都不理想。需要一种既能高效添加正相关延迟，又能避免上述问题的新方法。

Method: 提出自适应延迟相关性方法，以平均延迟、标准差（抖动）和半衰期参数为输入，为数据包添加正相关的延迟。实现基于DPDK的包延迟器和间隔器（DPDS），支持带宽限制、包乱序、流量监管和两种丢包模型。

Result: DPDS在硬件测试中表现优异：恒定延迟下零丢包吞吐量达95 Gbit/s，启用间隔功能时在3 ms抖动下达到85 Gbit/s；支持包乱序功能，恒定和变化延迟下零丢包吞吐量分别为73 Gbit/s和58 Gbit/s。性能显著优于NetEm和MoonEm。

Conclusion: 自适应延迟相关性方法成功解决了链路仿真中的延迟添加问题，避免了包乱序和延迟过度的问题。DPDS实现方案在性能上大幅超越现有链路仿真器，为高吞吐量网络环境下的链路仿真提供了有效解决方案。同时给出了半衰期参数的配置建议。

Abstract: In this paper we tackle the problem of adding varying delay to packets for link emulation. Naive approaches either add more delay than desired or cause packet reordering, both of which are undesirable. We develop adaptive delay correlation, which adds positively correlated delays to packets efficiently. It takes a mean delay and standard deviation (jitter) as input, as well as a half-life period to control the delay dynamics. We investigate the accuracy and dynamics of the resulting packet delays with and without bandwidth limitation. As a result we give a recommendation for the configuration of the half-life period. We implement adaptive delay correlation in a DPDK-based packet delayer and spacer (DPDS), investigate its performance on hardware, and compare it with the widely used link emulator NetEm and the recently developed DPDK-based emulator MoonEm. DPDS outperforms both of them with a zero-loss throughput of 95 Gbit/s for constant delay and, with spacing enabled, 85 Gbit/s for varying delay with 3 ms jitter. Further, DPDS supports packet reordering with zero-loss throughputs of 73 Gbit/s and 58 Gbit/s for constant and varying delay, respectively, as well as policing and two packet loss models.

</details>


### [179] [FlowCLIP: Contrastive Pretraining Using Domain Names for Encrypted Traffic Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17746)
*Eun Hun Choi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了FlowCLIP，一个针对加密流量域名预测的对比预训练框架，仅使用包到达间隔、包大小和包方向等侧信道特征，通过将原始域名作为文本监督信号，在QUIC流量数据集上实现了比竞争基线更好的跨时间泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络流量分类对网站指纹识别、入侵检测和服务质量管理至关重要，但在实际部署条件下开发能够捕获稳定且可泛化的流量模式的方法仍具挑战性，特别是针对加密流量的场景。

Method: 提出FlowCLIP框架：1）仅使用侧信道特征（包到达间隔时间、包大小、包方向）；2）将原始域名作为文本监督信号；3）采用CLIP风格的对比学习目标，对齐流量表示与域名表示；4）预训练后冻结流量编码器，通过线性探测在标准化域名标签上进行评估。

Result: 在大规模QUIC流量数据集上，采用基于时间的协议（Week 1训练，Weeks 2-4评估），FlowCLIP在后续评估周中均优于竞争性机器学习基线方法。

Conclusion: 原始域名可以提供有效的文本监督信号，用于学习可迁移的加密流量表示，证明了利用文本信息指导加密流量特征学习的有效性。

Abstract: Network traffic classification enables website fingerprinting, intrusion detection, and Quality of Service management. However, developing methods that capture stable and generalizable traffic patterns under realistic deployment conditions remains challenging. We introduce FlowCLIP, a contrastive pretraining framework for domain name prediction from encrypted traffic using only side-channel features: packet inter-arrival times, packet sizes, and packet directions. FlowCLIP uses raw domain names as textual supervision by aligning traffic flow representations with domain name representations through a CLIP-style contrastive objective. The pretrained traffic encoder is then frozen and evaluated through linear probing on canonicalized domain name labels. We evaluate FlowCLIP on a large-scale QUIC traffic dataset using a time-based protocol, where models are trained on Week 1 traffic and evaluated on traffic from Weeks 2-4. FlowCLIP outperforms competitive machine learning baselines across later evaluation weeks, suggesting that raw domain names provide a textual supervision signal for learning transferable encrypted traffic representations.

</details>


### [180] [UAV-CAS: A Calibrated Digital-Twin Dataset for Intrusion Detection in UAV Swarm Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17845)
*Sripath Mishra,Bharat Bhargava,Zizheng Liu,Shafkat Islam*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了UAV-CAS，一个大规模的UAV网络入侵检测数据集，通过Containernet数字孪生系统生成，并基于AERPAW测试平台测量进行系统校准。数据集包含99,492条流，涵盖五种攻击家族和九种协同攻击组合。实验表明，二分类攻击检测准确率超过0.98，但完整的攻击类型识别仍然困难，尤其是隐蔽攻击的F1分数极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于有线网络基准训练的入侵检测系统（IDS）在真实UAV集群环境中性能严重下降，原因是UAV的移动性、链路质量波动和去中心化路由导致流量分布发生变化。现有的UAV专用数据集未能系统性地覆盖这些条件变化，无法训练或测试IDS应对分布偏移的能力。

Method: 提出了UAV-CAS数据集，通过Containernet数字孪生系统生成，并设计了四层校准流水线：高度依赖的路径损耗、任务特定的移动性、链路级性能链和端到端轨迹保真度，基于AERPAW测试平台测量进行系统性校准。数据集包含99,492条流，覆盖1,024种配置，涵盖五种攻击家族（DoS、DDoS、黑洞、虫洞、重放）和九种协同攻击组合。

Result: 多样性分析显示高速攻击与良性流量的分离程度比以往基准高一个数量级，而隐蔽攻击则刻意与良性流量混合。在十个基线IDS上，二分类攻击检测准确率饱和于0.98以上，验证了数据集的可学习性；但完整攻击类别识别仍然困难，各类别F1分数从接近零到0.82不等，隐蔽攻击的F1分数降至个位数。

Conclusion: UAV-CAS数据集为UAV入侵检测研究提供了可复现的基础资源。研究公开了数据集、仿真器和校准数据，支持未来的UAV入侵检测研究。实验结果表明，虽然二分类检测任务已可解决，但多类别攻击识别特别是隐蔽攻击检测仍是待解决的难题。

Abstract: Intrusion detection systems (IDS) trained on wired-network benchmarks degrade sharply in real-world unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) swarms, where mobility, fluctuating link quality, and decentralized routing reshape traffic distributions. Existing UAV-specific datasets also do not systematically vary these conditions, leaving no way to train or test an IDS against the very shift that defeats it. We present UAV-CAS, a large-scale labeled flow dataset for UAV-network intrusion detection, generated by a Containernet digital twin that is systematically calibrated against AERPAW testbed measurements. We have a four-layer calibration pipeline spanning altitude-dependent path loss, mission-specific mobility, the link-level performance chain, and end-to-end trace fidelity. UAV-CAS comprises 99,492 flows drawn from 1,024 configurations that span five attack families (DoS, DDoS, blackhole, wormhole, replay) and nine collaborative attack compositions. A diversity analysis shows that high-rate attacks separate from benign traffic up to an order of magnitude more strongly than in any prior benchmark, while stealth attacks deliberately blend with benign traffic. Across ten baseline IDS, binary attack detection saturates above $0.98$, confirming the dataset is learnable, whereas full attack-class identification remains hard -- per-class $F_1$ ranges from near zero to $0.82$ and falls into the single digits for stealth attacks. We release the dataset, simulator, and calibration data to support reproducible UAV intrusion-detection research.

</details>


### [181] [Security-Induced Braess Paradoxes in Service Function Chain Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17987)
*Daniel Commey,Bin Mai*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文发现在服务功能链（SFC）编排中存在安全诱导的布雷斯悖论，即增加防御选项反而会恶化系统性能。作者提出了检测和缓解该悖论的方法，实验表明所提方法能将服务成本降低20-22%，攻击损失代理降低93.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络运营商通常认为增加安全防御选项（如虚拟防火墙、IDS/IPS副本、WAF集群等）是单调改进，即更多的检测能力、更低的延迟或更广泛的部署灵活性不应降低服务质量。然而，这种直觉可能失败，即使是局部有吸引力的新选项也可能导致全局性能恶化。作者旨在研究这种反直觉现象及其对网络安全管理的影响。

Method: 1）定义了布雷斯式安全管理动作的概念；2）在仿射负载依赖型VNF延迟下，推导了悖论出现的充分条件；3）提出了一种部署前编排筛选机制，能够拒绝、限制或预留有害选项；4）在四种拓扑场景（胖树数据中心、NSFNET风格WAN、GEANT风格WAN、边缘/雾拓扑）中进行了多租户SFC实验验证。

Result: 在理论识别的布雷斯悖论区域和默认参数下：1）朴素防御扩展使均衡服务成本增加27.2-30.8%，风险集中度增加6.1-9.7倍；2）悖论感知的约束使用将残余惩罚保持在1.9%以下；3）相比朴素扩展，服务成本降低20.0-22.1%；4）集中敏感型攻击损失代理平均降低93.5%。

Conclusion: 本文首次在SFC编排中识别并形式化了安全诱导的布雷斯悖论，证明了增加防御资源可能因流量集中和共享安全资源的对抗价值增加而适得其反。所提出的悖论感知编排方法能有效识别和缓解该问题，在保持安全性的同时显著降低服务成本和攻击风险。

Abstract: NFV/SDN orchestration lets operators instantiate and steer traffic through virtual firewalls, IDS/IPS replicas, WAF clusters, zero-trust gateways, backup inspection paths, and migration targets on demand. Operators often treat these options as monotone improvements: more inspection capacity, lower nominal latency, or broader placement flexibility should not degrade the service. That intuition can fail even when the new option is locally attractive. We study a security-induced Braess paradox in service function chain (SFC) orchestration, where adding a defensive option worsens the post-adaptation equilibrium by concentrating traffic and adversarial value on shared security resources. We define Braessian security-management actions, derive a sufficient condition for paradox emergence under affine load-dependent VNF delay, and give a pre-deployment orchestration screen that rejects, caps, or reserves harmful options. A multi-tenant SFC experiment suite applies the model to four topology-derived settings: a fat-tree datacenter, NSFNET-style WAN, GEANT-style WAN, and edge/fog topology. Under default parameters in the Braessian regime identified by the theory, naive defensive expansion raises equilibrium service cost by 27.2-30.8% and increases risk concentration by factors of 6.1-9.7. Paradox-aware constrained use keeps the residual penalty below 1.9%, reduces service cost by 20.0-22.1% relative to naive expansion, and lowers a concentration-sensitive attack-loss proxy by 93.5% on average.

</details>


### [182] [A T-API-Compliant ReAct Agentic Loop for Optical Networks: Generic vs. Domain-Specific Tool Abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18000)
*Seyed Morteza Ahmadian,Paolo Monti,Carlos Natalino*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了符合T-API标准的推理-行动(ReAct)闭环，用于光网络的意图驱动自治管理。实验表明，领域专用复合工具相比通用工具，在保持90%正确率（经oracle验证）的同时实现了三倍的token节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光网络需要意图驱动、闭环自治的管理机制，这是实现更高自治水平的关键使能技术。当前缺乏符合T-API标准的推理-行动闭环方案。

Method: 提出了首个符合T-API标准的ReAct(推理-行动)闭环框架。设计并实现了领域专用的复合工具，用于光网络的自动化管理。

Result: 领域专用复合工具相比通用工具实现了三倍的token节省，同时达到了90%的oracle验证正确率。

Conclusion: 证明了领域专用复合工具在光网络自治管理中的有效性，为光网络更高自治水平的实现提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Optical networks need intent-driven, closed-loop agentic management, a key enabler for higher autonomy levels. We present the first T-API-compliant reasoning and act (ReAct) loop. We show that domain-specific composite tools achieve 90% oracle-validated correctness with threefold token savings compared to generic tools.

</details>


### [183] [User-Mobility-Aware Optimization of Fiber Placement in Hybrid Fiber-IAB Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18016)
*Piotr Lechowicz,Charitha Madapatha,Carlos Natalino,Tommy Svensson,Paolo Monti*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对混合光纤-IAB网络的元启发式优化方法，通过将用户动态性纳入拓扑设计，实现了更具适应性和成本效益的回传架构，为6G网络基础设施的发展提供了可扩展和灵活的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着6G网络基础设施的发展，对可扩展和灵活的回传架构需求日益增长，现有拓扑设计方法未能充分考虑用户动态性，导致网络适应性不足和成本效率低下，因此需要新的优化方法来设计更具适应性的混合光纤-IAB网络。

Method: 采用元启发式优化算法对混合光纤-IAB网络进行拓扑设计，核心创新在于将用户动态性纳入优化框架，通过综合考虑用户行为模式和网络流量变化来优化网络拓扑结构。

Result: 将用户动态性纳入拓扑设计能够显著提升网络的适应性，实现更优的成本效率，为回传架构的设计提供了更灵活和可扩展的解决方案。

Conclusion: 集成用户动态性的混合光纤-IAB网络拓扑设计方法为构建可扩展、灵活且成本高效的6G网络基础设施提供了有效途径，证明了考虑动态因素在网络架构优化中的重要性。

Abstract: Metaheuristic optimization of hybrid fiber-IAB networks demonstrates that integrating user dynamics into topology design enables more adaptive and cost-efficient backhaul architectures, contributing to the development of scalable and flexible 6G network infrastructures.

</details>
